# Verified Claims Ledger

Claims ledger for the DF 53.16 world-generation reverse-engineering reports. Every claim below was re-examined by an independent auditor (and, where `confirmed_by` is 2, by a second skeptic) against the same binary and data files that the analyst used.

## Status key

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **verified** | Reproduced in this binary/data by both the analyst and the auditor. The cited addresses, constants and control flow were re-read and match the claim. |
| **public** | Well-known public knowledge (DF wiki, DFHack df-structures, raw files). Not verified here beyond consistency with what was found in the binary. |
| **inferred** | A plausible reading of the evidence, not proven. The mechanics cited are usually real, but a label, purpose or generalisation in the claim goes beyond what the evidence establishes, or the auditor downgraded it. |
| *refuted* | Listed separately per report. The claim as stated is contradicted by the binary; the verdict text records what the code actually does. |

Columns: `confidence` is the analyst's 0..1 self-estimate; `confirmed_by` is the number of independent reviewers who confirmed the claim without downgrading it (0, 1 or 2); `skeptic notes` are the reviewer verdicts verbatim (`confirmed:` / `downgrade:` / `refuted:`).

## params_rejection

**Title:** Parameter system, presets, and the rejection / validation loop  
**Report:** analyst report `params_rejection.md` (session working file, not committed; the ledger is the adjudicated record)  

**Summary:** The world_gen parameter set is one flat 0x4c8-byte struct; the writer at 0x140f80330 and loader at 0x140de5b10 give a complete token-to-offset map, enum encodings (pole, biome, field) and the semantics of *_RANGES (low/mid/high square minimums) and REGION_COUNTS (initial squares / initial regions / final regions). The ten presets are compiled into the binary: prefs/world_gen.txt is read first, otherwise presets are synthesised by an area-scaling default function (0x140f7f810, reference area 257^2) plus explicit ISLAND overrides. The map generator is a re-entrant sub-stage state machine (1..9); 53 numbered checks each consult a per-id allow flag, log to map_rejection_log.txt and return 1 (or 2 from the final region check). The caller resets the sub-stage to 2 (regenerate fields, RNG stream continues) or 1 (full re-init). "Shift down/up" re-biases elevation in place (+/-10, <=3 passes); "evenly distributed" means low/mid/high band minimums (33/66; 100/300 for elevation). UI maps 53 ids to 27 names via lookup tables.

### Refuted

- **`params_rejection-03`** (analyst status *verified*, confidence 0.93): Each *_FREQUENCY token holds one mesh-size index (0=Ignore, 1..5 = 2x2..32x32) followed by exactly five band weights; arrays of six ints sit at 0x400 (elevation), 0x418 (rain), 0x430 (drainage), 0x448 (savagery), 0x460 (temperature), 0x478 (volcanism).
  - Evidence: [BIN:func 0x140f808d6..0x140f80911] (mov edi,5 loop); editor strings 'Elevation Mesh Size','Ignore','2 x 2'..'32 x 32' [BIN:func 0x140de1a70]
  - Verdict: confirmed: Writer reads [rbp+0x400] then `mov edi,5` loop from 0x404; RAIN 0x418, DRAINAGE 0x430, TEMPERATURE 0x460, SAVAGERY 0x448, VOLCANISM 0x478 all reproduced; editor widget at 0x140de2524 binds param+0x400 to the option list Ignore, 2 x 2 .. 32 x 32 in that order; df-structures notes each *_frequency is 1 int plus 5.
  - Verdict: refuted: The storage layout (mesh at +0x400 then five weights, `mov edi,5` loop; arrays at 0x400/0x418/0x430/0x448/0x460/0x478 with the writer emitting TEMPERATURE(+0x460) before SAVAGERY(+0x448)) is correct, but the encoding is wrong: the editor widget bound to param+0x400 (0x140de2524..0x140de2622) has min 1, max 6 (r13d=6), sentinel value 1 displayed as 'Ignore', and pushes option values 2='2 x 2', 3='4 x 4', 4='8 x 8', 5='16 x 16', 6='32 x 32' — so 1=Ignore and 2..6 = 2x2..32x32 (meaning the default mesh 1 disables weighting, MEDIUM ISLAND's 4 is 8x8 and SMALL ISLAND's 3 is 4x4).
- **`params_rejection-11`** (analyst status *verified*, confidence 0.88): Default REGION_COUNTS (only for A>=65^2): initial squares SWAMP A/64, DESERT A/64, FOREST A/16, MOUNTAINS A/8, OCEAN A/8, GLACIER A/256, TUNDRA A/128, GRASSLAND A/8, HILLS A/8; initial regions 13/13/9/4/13/max(1,2)/6/9/9 scaled by A/66049; final regions 6/6/12/4/6/max(1,2)/3/12/12 scaled; glacier and tundra requirements are zeroed unless a dimension exceeds 257; for A<65^2 all RANGES and REGION_COUNTS are zero.
  - Evidence: [BIN:func 0x140f800d9..0x140f80308]
  - Verdict: refuted: Square fractions, the A<65^2 zeroing and the glacier/tundra zeroing unless a dim >257 reproduce, but tracing registers through 0x140f80094..0x140f80246 the initial-region multipliers are 7 (SWAMP,DESERT,OCEAN), 13 (FOREST,GRASSLAND,HILLS), 9 (MOUNTAINS), 4 (TUNDRA) and GLACIER = unclamped 2A/66049 (esi, never clamped), and final regions are 6/6/12/9/6/(2A/L)/3/12/12 (MOUNTAINS final 9), contradicting the claimed 13/13/9/4/13/max(1,2)/6/9/9 and final MOUNTAINS 4.
  - Verdict: refuted: The initial-square row (A/64, A/64, A/16, A/8, A/8, A/256, A/128, A/8, A/8) and the A<65^2 zeroing of REGION_COUNTS are right, but the raw code (`imul ecx,edi,0x7` at 0x140f80094 then multipliers 13, 9, 4, 6, 12, 3 at 0x140f8011c..0x140f8020f) gives initial regions SWAMP 7, DESERT 7, FOREST 13, MOUNTAINS 9, OCEAN 7, GLACIER max(1,scale 2), TUNDRA 4, GRASSLAND 13, HILLS 13 and final regions 6/6/12/9/6/max(1,2)/3/12/12 (MOUNTAINS final is 9, not 4); glacier/tundra are zeroed unless BOTH dims exceed 257 (`cmp ebp,0x101; jle` and `cmp r11d,0x101; jg` at 0x140f8024d), and for A<4225 ELEVATION_RANGES are not zeroed, only RAIN/DRAINAGE/SAVAGERY/VOLCANISM_RANGES and REGION_COUNTS.
- **`params_rejection-27`** (analyst status *inferred*, confidence 0.60): Indices 2 and 4 of the 24-slot field arrays are reserved internal map fields (likely GEOLOGY and an airmass/wind layer) and biome index 5 is LAKE; the globals 0x1424e5c04/08 passed to the map allocator are requested dimensions, so MT seeding happens before sub-stage 1 and in-map retries continue the same RNG stream.
  - Evidence: [INFERRED] from the 8-entry field jump table [BIN:func 0x140f8258c], token cluster [BIN:string 0x16a1a48..0x16a1ad0], biome table gap [BIN:func 0x140f825c4], allocator call [BIN:func 0x140f4a923..0x140f4a933] and the absence of RNG-wrapper calls in the worker/UI/init ranges
  - Verdict: downgrade: Biome 5 = LAKE and 0x1424e5c04/08 = requested dims are confirmed by public df-structures (world_region_type Lake=5; world_generatorst new_dimx/new_dimy) so they are public rather than inferred, but the index-2/4 guess is contradicted: worldgen_range_type (24 entries = int[24]) has 2 = VEGETATION and 4 = EVIL, GEOLOGY is the original name of DRAINAGE (5) and airmass/winds are 8-12; the absence of direct RNG-wrapper calls in the worker/UI/stage-1 ranges was reproduced but the seeding point remains an inference.
  - Verdict: refuted: Indices 2 and 4 are not GEOLOGY/airmass: the generator computes index 2 into tile+0x34 during the evenness pass (vegetation) and the enum gap matches df-structures' VEGETATION/EVILNESS, while biome 5 = LAKE is supported; the RNG part is right in conclusion but wrong in mechanism — the map stream is the vectorised multi-buffer MT at 0x140eb1820 (10-deep stack, selector 0x142152f70, depth 0x142354110), seeded by pushing a buffer from the SEED string (global copy +0x20 = 0x1424f36e8) via 0x140777ea0 inside 0x141438280, which the worker calls during setup at 0x140defca9 before any generator call, and popped only at the end of stage 9 (0x140f50587) or on abort (0x140df089a), so neither rejection path reseeds and retries continue the same stream.

### Skeptic extra findings

- 0x1424f36c8 is a whole-struct global copy of the active world_gen_param; every 'minimum copy' the report lists (0x1424f3798, 0x1424f379c, 0x1424f37a0, 0x1424f3828, 0x1424f3880.., 0x1424f3a44/48/4c, 0x1424f3b58) is just a field of it (+0xd0, +0xd4, +0xd8, +0x160, +0x1b8.., +0x37c/380/384, +0x490), and the error dump serialises this copy.
- Status block layout matches df-structures world_generatorst exactly: 0x1424e5a50 state, 0x1424e5a54 num_rejects, 0x1424e5a58 skip_reject[53], 0x1424e5b2c reject_type[53] (per-id counters incremented by the log writer), 0x1424e5c00 last_reject, 0x1424e5c04/08 new_dimx/new_dimy, 0x1424e6064 have_logged_parameters.
- Tile field offsets resolved from the evenness compares (answers a report open question): +0x30 elevation, +0x32 rainfall, +0x3a drainage, +0x3c volcanism, +0x3e savagery; the pass also writes +0x34 via 0x140f772a0 before classifying.
- Default REGION_COUNTS region multipliers in the report/claim 11 are wrong: initial regions scale 7/7/13/9/7/(2A/L unclamped)/4/13/13 and final 6/6/12/9/6/(same)/3/12/12 (consistent with the classic shipped LARGE REGION rows 1032:7:6, 4128:13:12, 8256:9:9).
- Peak detection mutates the field: tiles >=400 are clamped to 399 and tiles >=380 count as peaks; the report's '>=400 with secondary 380' is inverted.
- The edge-ocean 'partial' threshold is >= 1/8 of the edge's tiles below elevation 100; 'complete' is all of them.
- Allow flag for id 52 (BAD_FARMER) is set by 'ALLOW THIS REJECTION TYPE' but never read, so that rejection cannot actually be suppressed.
- Report cites [BIN:string 0x173ff90 '[DIM:'] but 0x173ff90 is 'NON_MOUNTAIN_CAVE_MIN:'; the DIM literal is 'DIM:' at fo 0x173ed90 (4 chars, below the strings -n 5 cutoff).
- POLE value 0 is serialised as the literal NONE, not omitted.
- worldgen_range_type (public, df-structures) is a 24-entry enum: 0 ELEVATION, 1 RAINFALL, 2 VEGETATION, 3 TEMPERATURE, 4 EVIL, 5 DRAINAGE(orig GEOLOGY), 6 VOLCANISM, 7 SAVAGERY, 8-14 AIRMASS_TEMP/X/Y, WEATHER, DAILY_WINDS, SNOWFALL, SALINITY, 15-23 unused; this explains the int[24] arrays and the skipped indices.
- Shift-down subtracts 10 from every tile below 300 including already-low ones; shift-up adds 10 only to tiles in [100,400).
- GOOD/EVIL_SQ_COUNTS small default is the unclamped 100A/66049 (same register as MOUNTAIN_CAVE_MIN before its clamp); the pocket special case triggers only when A == 17^2 exactly, and large is zeroed for A <= 33^2.
- TOTAL_CIV_NUMBER default has no floor above 129^2 and the ceiling 40 applies to all size classes; LOG_MAP_REJECTS is a YES/NO init flag (bit 12 of the init flag object at 0x14238ab78), not a level.
- The log writer does not store the message text in a global; it builds the log line from an existing global std::string at 0x142638848 and the param set (call 0x140a91760) and appends to the file.
- The generator's 'copies of active minimums' are fields of a single global world_gen_param instance at 0x1424f36c8 (ctor at 0x140a6d352, exported by '-world_gen_param' at 0x140f7dd3d, dumped on world-gen error at 0x140f500b1): +0xd0=0x1424f3798 PEAK, +0xd4/+0xd8=0x1424f379c/a0 edges, +0x160=0x1424f3828 SUBREGION_MAX, +0x1b8..=0x1424f3880.. four RANGES, +0x37c..=0x1424f3a44.. ELEVATION_RANGES, +0x490=0x1424f3b58 PS pointer.
- Mesh-size encoding is 1=Ignore, 2=2x2, 3=4x4, 4=8x8, 5=16x16, 6=32x32 (editor widget 0x140de2524..0x140de2622 bound to +0x400, option values pushed 2..6, sentinel 1 -> 'Ignore'); the default presets therefore ship with weighting disabled, and the report's MEDIUM ISLAND '16x16' is 8x8 and SMALL ISLAND '8x8' is 4x4.
- REAL_WORLD_EXTINCT encoding from the writer's compare chain 0x140f80c0e..0x140f80c50: 0=EXTINCT, 1=ISOLATED, 2=UNTAMED_WILDS (the dimension default), 3=WILDERNESS, 4=DOMESTICATED — the report's open question ('1 = DOMESTICATED') is wrong.
- Default REGION_COUNTS region rows are initial 7/7/13/9/7/max(1,2)/4/13/13 and final 6/6/12/9/6/max(1,2)/3/12/12 (multipliers at 0x140f80094..0x140f8020f), matching the 0.47 shipped file's 1032:7:6 / 4128:13:12 / 8256:9:9 shape; glacier/tundra are zeroed unless both dimensions exceed 257.
- Peak placement (sub-stage 3, 0x140f4ccf0..0x140f4d104): clamp all tiles >=400 to 399, collect candidates >=380, pick the first at random (0x140071ec0 -> 0x140eb1820) and the rest farthest-first by Manhattan distance, set each to 400, flag bit 13 on tile+0x20 and push a 0x98-byte object (coords at +0x78/+0x7a) onto world+0x280; shift-up clamps at 399 so these stay the only 400 tiles.
- Tile record layout (0x70 bytes): +0x30 elevation, +0x32 rainfall, +0x34 vegetation (written by 0x140f772a0 during the evenness pass), +0x36 temperature, +0x38 evilness, +0x3a drainage, +0x3c volcanism, +0x3e savagery — the report's open question swaps savagery/volcanism; this is the df-structures region_map_entry order and explains the int[24]/8-entry field enum with holes at 2 and 4.
- RNG architecture missed by the report and the brief: a vectorised MT19937 genrand at 0x140eb1820 (SSE masks at 0x1417869c0.., scalar fallback 0x140eb1b06 with 0x9908b0df when CPU level <2) over a stack of up to 10 state buffers (stride 0x9c0 at 0x142288880, index array 0x14212ba18, selector WORD 0x142152f70, depth WORD 0x142354110, per-slot type array 0x14234ea00); helpers: 0x140eb17a0 init-from-GetTickCount, 0x140eb1bf0 push seeded by value, 0x140eb1cb0 push seeded by three values, 0x140777ea0 push seeded from a string (calls 0x140529a40), 0x140eb1bc0 pop. The brief's 0x1405xxxxx 'wrapper family' are scalar MT seeders (all contain the 0x6c078965 init multiplier) and are not used by the map generator.
- Worldgen seeding chain: worker 0x140deea70 -> 0x141438280 (world init, logs 'Creature Seed:' to gamelog) -> 0x140777ea0(push, seed string at 0x1424f36e8 = param copy +0x20); the Lua generate entry 0x140eca3a0 pushes its own buffer at 0x140eca458 and pops at 0x140eca84b, so Lua object generation does not perturb the map stream; the generator pops at the end of stage 9 (0x140f50587) and the cave/civ function pushes/pops a sub-stream around civ placement (pop at 0x140f66faf); the input handler pops on abort keys at 0x140e02d81.
- Rejection bookkeeping: besides the allow array 0x1424e5a58[53], the log writer keeps a per-id rejection count array at 0x1424e5b2c[53]; LOG_MAP_REJECTS is a boolean init flag (bit 12 of the flagarray at 0x14238ab78), not a level; after each rejection the worker sets g_1424e5c14 = max(500, 4*sub_140e0b540()) — the '500 comparison' in the report's open questions is this delay/timer value.
- Sub-stage 1 calls 0x141438160 on the status block, which clears a counter at +0xbf8 and a series of vectors at +0xc00..+0xcc8 (not the allow flags), so the worldgen status object is at least 0xcd0 bytes and allow flags persist across retries; status text also has a state>=8 threshold at 0x140df51b2 beyond the seven listed.
- The loader does not write synthesised presets back to prefs/world_gen.txt (both path references precede parsing); the stage worker's four consecutive 0x14052cb00 calls at 0x140defc69..0x140defc99 are std::string single-char assignments, not seed conversions.
- Shift-down/shift-up detail: the 'medium after shift' checks (ids 2 and 4) are only evaluated when the three-pass budget is exhausted, and the high count is carried across passes while low/mid are recounted each pass.

### Open questions (carried from the report)

- Where the MT seed is applied for the map pass and whether a code-2 (final region) restart draws a fresh seed; no calls to the identified RNG wrapper family were found in the worker, UI driver or sub-stage-1 init block.
- Exact per-tile int16 offsets for rainfall/drainage/savagery/volcanism (+0x32/+0x3a/+0x3c/+0x3e observed) and the meaning of the secondary 380 peak threshold.
- Whether the loader writes synthesised presets back to prefs/world_gen.txt (two path strings built; only the read path was traced).
- Meaning of the 500 (0x1f4) comparison after a rejection in the worker and the role of helper 0x140795fa0 on a final-check rejection.
- Full value encoding of REAL_WORLD_EXTINCT beyond value 1 = DOMESTICATED (default 2).
- How the 'partial' edge-ocean threshold fraction is computed (compared against a derived value, not read directly).

## elevation_fields

**Title:** Fractal field generation: elevation, rainfall, temperature, drainage, volcanism, savagery (+ poles, peaks, ocean edges, orographic rain)  
**Report:** analyst report `elevation_fields.md` (session working file, not committed; the ledger is the adjudicated record)  

**Summary:** All seven climate fields (elevation, rainfall, temperature, drainage, volcanism, savagery, plus a hidden fixed-range 33..65 evilness slot) are generated at world-tile resolution by one recursive square-subdivision midpoint-displacement routine (0x140f554a0) driven from the map generator (0x140f4a790). A coarse-to-fine mesh pass seeds grid points from a five-bucket weighted range, borders are generated first as 1-D fractal lines and forced to elevation 50 to satisfy PARTIAL/COMPLETE_OCEAN_EDGE_MIN (partial = >=1/8 of an edge <100, complete = all), with a retry loop; the interior is filled square by square. Temperature is a fractal field plus a quadratic latitude term chosen by POLE. Peaks are elevation >=380 (>=400 clamped to 399); bands are <100 ocean, >=300 mountain with up to three +/-10 shifts; other fields are bucketed at 33/66. Later passes: latitude-band wind table driving orographic rain (+/-10 per uplift credit), a BFS coast-to-foothill ramp ('Mountain-Slope'), and 1%-chance cliff flattening.

### Refuted

- **`elevation_fields-21`** (analyst status *inferred*, confidence 0.75): The map generator is re-entered per worldgen stage (global short 0x1424e5a50); field generation plus the latitude, peak, band, distribution and volcano checks run at stage 3, while erosion, orographic rain and the slope ramp run at stage 4; 'Setting temperature...' is a cumulative UI label, not a separate temperature function.
  - Evidence: [BIN:func 0x140f4ca7c] cmp 3; [BIN:func 0x140f4e1c4] cmp 4; stage writes [BIN:func 0x140df09c8/0x140df0a24]; UI labels [BIN:func 0x140df4ba2-0x140df4c2b]
  - Verdict: refuted: Re-entry per stage is real (reads of 0x1424e5a50 at 0x140f4a7f4/0x140f4aaf7/0x140f4ca62/0x140f4e1b6/0x140f4f1db, increments at 0x140f4effe/0x140f5058c/0x140f506eb) and the UI labels are cumulative, but the report missed 'cmp r10w,2; jne' at 0x140f4ab05: grid clearing, mesh seeding, borders and the interior fractal run at stage 2, the latitude/peak/band/distribution/volcano checks at stage 3, erosion at stage 4 (block ends with the inc at 0x140f4effe), 0x140f53750 plus the river generator at stage 5 (cmp 5 at 0x140f4f0bd), and both slope calls plus the orographic sweep at stage 6 (cmp 6 at 0x140f4f1e9, block to 0x140f50239) - two of the three stage assignments are wrong.
  - Verdict: refuted: The stage assignments are off by one or two: 0x140f4ab05 `cmp stage,2` guards the whole field block (full sentinel clear, setup, mesh seeding, borders, interior fill, 0x140f4ab10-0x140f4ca62), 0x140f4ca7c `cmp 3` guards latitude/peaks/bands/distribution/vegetation/volcano checks, 0x140f4e1c4 `cmp 4` the erosion cycles (ending with `inc word [stage]` at 0x140f4effe), 0x140f4f0bd `cmp 5` the river helpers and river generator, and 0x140f4f1e9 `cmp 6` the slope ramp, orographic sweep, second slope call, subregion check and param dump; the generator runs one stage per call, increments the stage in its common tail (0x140f506eb) and returns 0, while a rejection returns 1 (0x140f4e04e) and the worker resets the stage to 2 and bumps 0x1424e5a54 (0x140df09ab-0x140df0a24); the cumulative-label part (stage>=1 'Preparing elevation...', >=2 'Setting temperature...', >=3 'Running rivers...') holds.

### Skeptic extra findings

- Several [BIN:string] tags in the report (0x173f140 'Not enough peaks:', 0x1740af0 'Mountain-Slope Overflow', 0x173ff08 '[ELEVATION_FREQUENCY:') are RVAs (VA-0x140000000), not strings_ascii.txt file offsets; the file offsets are 0x173ef40, 0x17408f0, 0x173ed08.
- Stage map is wrong in the report: stage 2 = clear grid + mesh seeding + borders + interior fractal (shown under 'Setting temperature...'), stage 3 = latitude/peak/band/distribution/volcano checks, stage 4 = erosion (0x140f52100, erode-extremes, 0x140f549b0), stage 5 = 0x140f53750/0x140f530f0 + river generator, stage 6 = Mountain-Slope (twice: 0x140f4f205, 0x140f4f85e) + orographic sweep. Section 6's '(0x1424e5a50 == 4)' heading does not cover orographic/slope.
- The param+0x490 override table is DF's pre-set ('painted') map feature: the writer emits it as PS_EL/PS_RF/PS_TP/PS_DR/PS_VL/PS_SV at 0x140f82427-0x140f824c8, and the stage-2 clear at 0x140f4ab54-0x140f4ab7e copies each supplied map (array of column pointers to shorts) into the slot instead of writing -30000. This resolves the report's open question and gives slot 0/1/3/5/6/7 = EL/RF/TP/DR/VL/SV in-binary.
- The full-grid sentinel clear is at 0x140f4ab10-0x140f4adde (stage-2 entry), not at 0x140f4b3e0-0x140f4bb42, which clears only the border rows/columns; the edge-ocean retry jumps to that border clear and then falls through into the whole mesh-seeding loop, so every coarse seed of every slot is re-rolled on each retry, not just the borders.
- The interior variance block [rbp+0x270] is a copy of X-Variance when W-1<=H-1 and of Y-Variance otherwise (0x140f4b1c0-0x140f4b26f), not a 'combined' block; for square worlds only X-Variance ever shapes the interior.
- Weighted-range boundaries use /5 (0x66666667 sar 1), not '/10' as the report says; the 20/40/60/80 percent result is unchanged.
- Tile flags at +0x20 are a bit-array object (data pointer at +0x20, byte count at +0x28; 0x1400fb7e0/0x140071f20 bounds-check idx>>3 against it), not a DWORD; bit 13 is an index into that array.
- Orographic 'rising' is measured against the elevation of the last tile that received uplift (di updated only on uplift and reset to 0 once the credit reaches 0), not against the previous tile; the first uplift tile adds 0 rain because c is added before being incremented.
- ELEVATION_RANGES is written in the order +0x380,+0x37c,+0x384 = low,mid,high (and RAIN_RANGES +0x1bc,+0x1b8,+0x1c0 = low,mid,high), consistent with the generator comparing 0x1424f3a48 to the low count and 0x1424f3a44 to the mid count; the report's open question on this order is answerable.
- The shift-up pass adds 10 to every non-ocean tile below 400 (cap 399), not only to tiles below 300 as the algorithm sketch states; shift-down applies to tiles below 300 with floor 1.
- The 'RANGES tokens 0x140f81700+' citation points at the 10-iteration REGION_COUNTS writer loop (DESERT/FOREST... strings, stride 0x28); the *_RANGES writer is at 0x140f8219x-0x140f823fe.
- In the wind helper, pole mode -1 (NONE) takes the same fold path as mode 2 (both poles), so the latitude-band wind table still applies when no pole is set.
- The UI driver cumulative label order is Preparing elevation (stage>=1), Setting temperature (>=2), Running rivers (>=3), Forming lakes (>=4), and minerals (>=5), Growing vegetation (>=6), Verifying terrain (>=7), Importing wildlife (>=8), which lags the generator's actual stage work by one or more stages.
- Stage map (corrects report section 5/6 and the 'Setting temperature' discussion): stage 1 = init (0x141438160 resets the status object at 0x1424e5a50) + Lua procedural generate (0x140eca3a0); stage 2 = sentinel clear, parameter setup, mesh seeding, border lines, edge-ocean retry, volcanism hotspots, interior fractal fill; stage 3 = latitude, peaks, bands/shifts, edge-ocean rejection, 33/66 distribution checks, vegetation seed, volcano count; stage 4 = region-count check ('X region count fails by' strings 0x140f4e303-0x140f4e5cb), 'Not enough river start points before erosion', erosion cycles via 0x140f52100/0x140f549b0, PERIODICALLY_ERODE_EXTREMES, a flag-5 pass, then inc stage; stage 5 = per-tile 0x140f53750/0x140f530f0 and the river generator 0x140f772f0; stage 6 = Mountain-Slope (0x140f4f205), peak-list pass over world+0x280, orographic sweep, Mountain-Slope again (0x140f4f85e), 'Too many distinct subregions', param dump 0x140f80330, 0x140f8a020; stages 7-9 follow (0x140f5058c inc after 0x141508be0/0x1415088d0; 0x140f50425 cmp 9).
- The global at 0x1424e5a50 is the base of a worldgen status struct, not a lone short: +0 stage, +4 counter incremented by the worker on every rejection (0x140df09ab), and a run of per-rejection 'ignore' DWORD flags (+8 peaks 0x1424e5a58, +0xc medium, +0x10 medium-after-down, +0x14 low-after-down, 0x1424e5a74 rain, a78 drainage, a7c savagery, a80 volcanism, aa8 volcanoes); the report's single 'rejections disabled switch' inference generalises to one switch per rejection type.
- Orographic rain runs after river tracing (stage 6 vs stage 5), so rivers are traced on pre-orographic rainfall; the slope ramp also runs twice in stage 6, once before the peak-list pass and once after the orographic sweep.
- Mesh seeding uses a two-pass diamond-square stencil per level (pass 0: points at odd x/even y from the two horizontal neighbours and even x/odd y from the two vertical neighbours; pass 1: odd/odd centres from the four edge-adjacent neighbours N/S/E/W at distance step, 0x140f4bf83-0x140f4c062), whereas the recursive routine 0x140f554a0 takes its centre from the four square corners; the report describes only the corner stencil.
- The edge-ocean retry (jump to 0x140f4b3e0/0x140f4b3e3) re-enters before the mesh-seeding loop, and the seeding writes (0x140f4bc6c, 0x140f4be4b, 0x140f4c0f4) carry no sentinel check, so every mesh point is re-rolled on a retry; the retry loop has no iteration cap.
- Mesh widget labels: value 1 = 'Ignore' (0x140de2553), 2 = '2 x 2', 3 = '4 x 4', 4 = '8 x 8', 5 = '16 x 16' (0x140de2560-0x140de25fb; '32 x 32' = 6 by extension), resolving the report's open question; 'Ignore' means uniform corner seeds with no weighted levels, and it is also the setting that enables the volcanism hotspot seeding and the edge-ocean retry.
- Peak selection: after a random first pick, each further peak maximises its minimum Manhattan distance to the chosen set (farthest-point sampling); chosen tiles are raised to 400 while everything else was clamped to 399, so elevation 400 is the unique chosen-peak marker in addition to flag bit 13, and a 0x98-byte peak record (x at +0x78, y at +0x7a, initialised through 0x140a8f880 with type 0x13) is appended to world+0x280.
- Orographic reference semantics: the comparison elevation is the last tile that received uplift, not the immediately previous tile; it is left unchanged on non-rising tiles while credit > 0 (so the whole lee slope burns credit against the ridge height) and reset to 0 when credit is exhausted (so any tile >=150 or drainage>=50 restarts accumulation with +0 rain and c=1); the first tile of each row is skipped and all rows are swept regardless of wind sign.
- Wind helper pole handling: mode -1 (no poles) uses the same equator fold as mode 2 (both poles), so a POLE:NONE world still gets a two-hemisphere wind pattern (polar easterlies at both edges, westerlies mid-hemisphere, trades near the equator); mode 1 (south) mirrors the row, mode 0 (north) uses it directly.
- Interior variance block selection: the +0x270 block is a copy of the X-variance block when W-1 <= H-1 and of the Y-variance block otherwise (0x140f4b1c0-0x140f4b26f); evil's X-block entry is W*200/257 and its Y-block entry H*200/257, so for a non-square world the interior evil variance follows the smaller dimension.
- Band shifts: shift-down touches only tiles <300 (floor 1), shift-up touches only tiles in [100,400) (cap 399); chosen peaks at 400 are untouched by both, and ocean is never raised by shift-up.
- World-tile flags at +0x20 are a bit-array object (byte pointer + byte count, DFHack flagarray style), accessed with bounds-checked set/test helpers 0x1400fb7e0/0x140071f20/0x1400fb810; stage 4 also sets bit 5 on tiles (0x140f4efd3).
- Field RNG: 0x140eb1820 is Michael Brundage's MT variant (byte-indexed counter, no tempering, returns raw state words) with several 624-word buffers selected by the short at 0x142152f70 and per-buffer indices at 0x14212ba18; the generator also calls it directly at 0x140f4a9f0; the 0x14050d110 twist with the 0x4e0 double buffer belongs to a different RNG family, so the brief's 'MT anchor' is not the stream the field generator consumes.
- Override table at param+0x490: entry k lives at +8+8*k (the first qword is skipped, likely a vtable or header) and is a short** column array copied into slot k during the stage-2 clear (0x140f4ab60-0x140f4ab7e), so this is a working import path for painted field maps; it also disables the edge, band and distribution checks and the slope ramp for that slot.
- Writer/editor binding: [FIELD:min:max:xvar:yvar] is emitted from +0x1e8/+0x248/+0x2a8/+0x308 (0x140f80844-0x140f80875) and the editor widgets bind ' X-Variance' to +0x2a8 and ' Y-Variance' to +0x308 with range 0..3200 (0x140de23a8-0x140de2446), which fixes the X/Y identity used in claim 7.
- Mountain-Slope uses 8-connectivity (3x3 loops) for both seeding and BFS expansion, and the two 120000-entry buffers are used ping-pong (720000 shorts at 0x141bad5f0), so 'a 120000-entry static queue' is really two wavefront buffers of that size.

### Open questions (carried from the report)

- Exact mapping between the editor labels '2 x 2'..'32 x 32' and the stored mesh exponent (likely label = 2^(m-1) squares, so '2 x 2' = m 2) was not traced in the editor code.
- Writer order of the three ELEVATION_RANGES values versus the +0x37c/+0x380/+0x384 layout (only the first load was captured).
- What produces the per-slot override table at param +0x490 (0x1424f3b58): an import/painted-map feature or debugging hook?
- Whether slots 8..23 of the per-tile field array are ever generated or are padding (loops run to 24 but no parameters map to them).
- Characterisation of the erosion helpers 0x140f52100 and 0x140f549b0 and the stage-4 feature-type switch (+0x7c, 10 cases) was left to the erosion/rivers analyst.
- Whether disabling the edge-ocean retry when the elevation mesh exceeds 1 is deliberate design or a leftover.

## hydrology

**Title:** Hydrology: rivers (loop-erased walks), erosion cycles, lakes, drainage, salinity  
**Report:** analyst report `hydrology.md` (session working file, not committed; the ledger is the adjudicated record)  

**Summary:** DF 53.16 hydrology is a three-phase pipeline inside the stage-machine map generator. Stage 4 runs EROSION_CYCLE_COUNT cycles: each cycle scans mountain tiles (elev >= 150) as river-start candidates (rejecting below RIVER_MINS[0]), runs RIVER_MINS[1] exploratory random strictly-downhill walks that carve the lowest neighbour down to current-1 when stuck (this is the erosion), plus an optional 1%-chance cliff clip of +15; the last cycle sweeps every land tile from the top elevation down, running final-mode rivers that write has_river and four link bits and leave sink marks where they bottom out at sea level. Stage 5 grows lakes from sinks by priority flood, smooths by a majority fixpoint, then the 'Loop Erase River' routine extracts ordered rivers from the link graph with random fork choice, loop erasure on self-revisit, rainfall-sum flow, tributary merging with trunk swap, naming and mouth-first topological sort. Biome/salinity tokens verified only as strings.

### Kept claims

| id | status | confidence | confirmed_by | claim | evidence | skeptic notes |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|---|---|

### Refuted

- None.

### Skeptic extra findings

- Major River threshold is 20000 (cmp DWORD [rax],0x4e20 at 0x14080d204 before 'Major River: '), answering the report's open question; df-structures' world_river comment agrees (River 10000-19999).
- Brook vs Stream (flow < 5000) is decided by tile flag bit 15 via 0x1400bbf90(world,x,y,0xf) at 0x14080d2f8-0x14080d314 — bit 15 is is_brook/BROOK in df-structures — not by a lower flow threshold; the report's 'brook/stream split' open question is answered.
- df-structures (Bay12 original names) confirms every bit role used in the report: 0 RIVER, 2 NEWLAKE, 3 SITE, 4 TEMPRIVER, 5-8 RIVER_N/S/E/W, 13 PEAK, 14 LAKE, 15 BROOK, 16 CONSTRUCTION, 17 ORIGINAL_RIVER_SOURCE; the report's [INFERRED] bit roles can be upgraded to public-corroborated.
- The river object has a second per-tile int16 vector at +0xd8 (df-structures 'elevation', original 'exit_elevation') that map-generator stage 6 fills at 0x140f4ff84-0x140f4ff96; the 'Error sorting rivers' check at 0x140f4ffc4-0x140f50026 fires when the downstream river (by end pos via 0x140f84270) still has an empty +0xd8 vector, i.e. it has not been processed yet; +0xc0 is exit_tile (0-15).
- Elevation-unit open question: df-structures' region_map_entry.elevation comment states PSV elevation uses 100-299 for normal biomes 'with range later cut to 1/4, and Mountains shifted down' giving 0-99 ocean, 100-149 other, 150+ mountains — a [PUBLIC] answer to the rescale between the 0..400 field stage and the river stage.
- Post-erosion band re-check is in stage 7 (after cmp r10w,0x7 at 0x140f50239) and the river-sort check in stage 6 (cmp r10w,0x6 at 0x140f4f1e9); stage 5 processes one column of sinks per tick (0x1424e5c0c vs 0x1424e5c04) before the fixpoint and the single loop-erase call.
- Post-cycle strip makes 9 clear-bit calls for a river tile (bits 0,5,6,7,8 on the tile plus reciprocal 7/8/6/5 on W/E/N/S neighbours) plus one for bit 4 — the report says eight.
- Lake flood detail missed: growth stops immediately if the frontier contains an ocean (<100) or existing-lake tile that is not sink/visited; 0x140f533c0 drives a growth fixpoint absorbing adjacent has_river tiles (not the trim), 0x140f54610 gates the trim, and the trim bound is members/2 - dry_members/2.
- The 'Not enough river start points before erosion' rejection is bypassed when the per-slot override table pointer 0x1424f3b58 is non-null with a non-zero second field, or when rejection-disable flag 0x1424e5ad0 is set.
- The candidate-scan loop also records the lowest non-peak tile in the interior (x,y within the middle half of the map) into globals 0x1424e60b0/0x1424e60b4, defaulting to the map centre — unreported, likely a later placement anchor.
- The cited BFS range 0x140f775e0-0x140f77bbf contains out-of-line Pass B blocks (0x140f777b3-0x140f77904) that set/clear link bits; control flow (jne from 0x140f77459, jmp back to 0x140f7748d) shows they belong to the reciprocal-link pass, so Pass B's address should be cited as ~0x140f7745x-0x140f77904 rather than inside the BFS.
- Per the decompile, the trace-start neighbour scan checks the W, N and E neighbours for bit 17 but breaks before the S neighbour; this may be a decompiler artifact and was not confirmed in raw code (low confidence).
- 0x140a8f8e0 (river naming) references no strings itself; the 'language tables' attribution rests only on the two globals (g_1424ea048, g_1424eecc8) passed to it.
- Candidate scan and the 'Not enough river start points before erosion' rejection execute only on the first stage-4 tick (the total==0 branch at 0x140f4e1d5 jumps past them when total!=0); later cycles draw exploratory starts from the frozen pre-erosion candidate list even if those tiles have since been eroded. The report's per-cycle list and algorithm sketch wrongly recompute starts every cycle.
- The first stage-4 tick also calls the region builder 0x140f52100 and runs the '<Biome> region count fails by' checks (strings at 0x140f4e303-0x140f4e5cb) before the candidate scan; the rejection is skipped when the param+0x490 override table has entry [+8] non-null or global 0x1424e5ad0 != 0.
- The candidate-scan loop doubles as a search for the highest non-peak tile within the central half of the map (x in (w/4, 3w/4), y in (h/4, 3h/4)), stored in globals 0x1424e60b0/0x1424e60b4 (default w/2,h/2) and read by the worldgen UI at 0x140df478c and 0x140df73e7 — probably the map-view focus point.
- No elevation rescale exists between stages: the biome classifier 0x140f51940 (used by the region builder and hence the final region checker, which has no 150/300 compares of its own) defines MOUNTAIN as elevation >= 150 and ocean as < 100 (27 tropical / 28 temperate / 29 arctic by temperature <= -5); the pre-erosion 'high elevation' band (>= 300 at 0x140f4d104) and the post-erosion 'high' band (>= 150 at 0x140f50265) are compared against the same parameter minimums at param+0x37c/+0x380/+0x384.
- Lake salinity is traced: 0x140f51940 reads a per-tile salinity value at tile+0x4c (slot 14) and maps bit-14 tiles to LAKE biomes 36..41 with >= 66 salt, >= 33 brackish, else fresh; the saltwater outcome has nothing to do with the dry-member trimming in the lake routine.
- Lake formation details missing from the report: the priority flood terminates on first frontier contact with ocean or a finished lake (no water-level rule), a majority-rule absorption of adjacent river tiles (0x140f533c0) follows, trimming removes the highest 2*elev-rain tiles (eligibility 0x140f54610: interior tile, no river at x+-1, local membership pattern) until fewer than N/2 - Ndry/2 remain, thin or border lake tiles are demoted to river tiles with links to adjacent lake/river/ocean, and the lake bit is set only where tile temperature (+0x36) >= 0, so freezing basins get no lakes.
- 0x140f79fe0 (the report's open question) is a brook generator: it collects every path tile of every river, repeatedly picks one at random (0x140eb1820), grows a walk from it with step helper 0x140f95cd0 (which tracks the maximum neighbouring elevation, i.e. climbs uphill, with RNG tie-breaking), marks each visited tile has_river (bit 0) + bit 15 + link bits, then creates a new 264-byte river object spliced to the parent river found by 0x140f84270 and names it with 0x140a8f8e0. Bit 15 is the is_brook flag the embark screen tests via 0x1400bbf90(world,x,y,15).
- Trace-start neighbour scan quirk (raw 0x140f77d44-0x140f77dbb): for dx=0 the inner dy loop breaks at dy=0, so only the west, north and east neighbours are examined for the bit-17 / processed tests; a river tile whose only headwater neighbour is to the south is never chosen as a trace start.
- Tiles with elevation >= 250 are inert in stage 4: the river walk's lowest-neighbour search uses a 250 sentinel so they are never carved, and the final sweep starts at min(elevation max, 249) so they never start a final river; combined with the >= 150 strip pass this is why river heads appear at mountain margins.
- 0x140eb1820 is an inlined MT19937 (624-word twist with 0x9908b0df, state index advanced by 4 bytes per call) used game-wide (5737 call sites); the 1/500 test reduces its output modulo 0x7fffffff before comparing with 0x418938, and all uniform picks use the (0x7fffffff / n) + 1 divisor idiom.
- Helper identities worth recording: 0x1400fb7e0 set-bit, 0x1400fb810 clear-bit, 0x140071f20 test-bit, 0x140071ec0 random(n) (used for the 1/100 cliff test and the random-order sweeps), 0x14006f3e0 vector size, 0x1400fb4a0 vector erase, 0x140f7b030 elevation-aware river lookup used by the brook generator.
- The final sweep's per-level cap is effectively 1001 tiles (count incremented before the cmp 0x3e8 / jg), a trivial off-by-one versus the report's 1000.

### Open questions (carried from the report)

- Elevation units differ between the field stage (0..400, mountains >= 300 per the elevation lane) and the erosion/river stage (sentinel 250, sweep capped at 249, mountain at 150); is there a rescale between stages or do the stages use different bands?
- Exact semantics of the lake trim bound and helpers 0x140f533c0 / 0x140f54610, and where per-tile salinity (fresh/brackish/salt for lakes and rivers) is written.
- The brook/stream split (flag or lower flow threshold) and the major-river threshold, cut off in the examined view of 0x14080ae60.
- What 0x140f79fe0 and the river +0xc0 vector compute (exit sub-tile positions vs something else).
- Why the sink-reachability BFS result in the loop-erase routine is discarded (vestigial code or decompiler/disassembly blind spot).

---

## biomes_regions

**Title:** Biome derivation, regions/subregions, good/evil/savagery alignment, regional interactions  
**Report:** analyst report `biomes_regions.md` (session working file, not committed; the ledger is the adjudicated record)  

**Summary:** Biome is never stored: a 1.2 KB classifier (0x140f51940) derives one of 42 world-tile biomes from elevation (≥150 mountain, <100 ocean), temperature (≤−5 freezing; taiga <10; broadleaf ≥65), rainfall bands 10/20/33/66/75, drainage 10/33/50/66/75, salinity 33/66, a lake flag, and a latitude-based tropical test with a rainfall-modulated blend helper. A pre-compression twin uses ≥300; elevation is compressed (≥300 → −150; 100–299 → 100–149 at 0.25) right after the initial square counts. Wrappers map biomes to 10 region kinds (hills = grassland with drainage ≥50). Regions are 4-connected same-kind label-propagation components; landmasses 8-connected non-ocean; regions count only at ≥35 tiles (mountains ≥20 lowland-bordering), lakes never; SUBREGION_MAX caps the region vector. Good/evil placement buckets regions by size (1/25/100), spends GOOD/EVIL square budgets, balances across kinds, sets is_evil/is_good, re-rolls evilness 66–100 / 0–32, and rebuilds populations from EVIL/GOOD raws. Savagery/evilness labels are a 3×3 grid at 33/66.

### Refuted

- **`biomes_regions-24`** (analyst status *inferred*, confidence 0.50): The 'surrounded by the ocean' requirement for dwarf placement could not be located in the binary; the civilisation placer shows no direct landmass-vector access, so the mechanism (landmass area or local flood) remains public/unverified.
  - Evidence: [BIN:func 0x140f60c30] string and offset scan; help text [BIN:string 0x1719df0]; [PUBLIC] wiki
  - Verdict: refuted: The supporting observation is false: 0x140f60c30 uses r14 as the world pointer ([r14+0x2b0] at 0x140f60f58, [r14+0xa0]/[r14+0xa4]) and references the landmass vector world+0x220 at 0x140f61cb1, 0x140f61d4c, 0x140f66d50 and 0x140f6a515 (`lea rcx,[r14+0x220]; call 0x14006ede0` = vector size), the first one immediately after the good/evil call; the ocean-surround rule is indeed still unlocated, but 'shows no direct landmass-vector access' must be dropped and those sites are the lead.
  - Verdict: refuted: The premise is wrong: the civ placer 0x140f60c30 accesses the landmass vector world+0x220 at 0x140f61cb1, 0x140f61d4c, 0x140f66d50, 0x140f6a515, 0x140f6a616 and 0x140f6a667, builds a per-landmass tally of candidate points (lists 0x1424e64f8/0x1424e6510) immediately after the good/evil call and tracks the landmass with the maximum, and stores the landmass pointer into a per-site record at 0x140f66d5f — the natural place for the island/ocean rule, so it is locatable, not absent.

### Skeptic extra findings

- Savanna '6' anomaly consequence is inverted in the report: blend<=6 is stricter than blend<66, so savanna becomes tropical only deeper in the zone (narrower band), not almost everywhere.
- Third kind wrapper 0x140f52000 (used by the subregion builder for region connectivity and the landmass test) maps lake biomes 36-41 to kind 4 (ocean); its table has no '5' case. Hence lakes are holes in landmasses, lake and ocean tiles merge into one connectivity component, and a region's stored kind (via 0x140f51de0, lake=5) is taken from the first scanned tile.
- Pool biomes 30-35 fall to the default case (kind 3, mountains) in all three kind wrappers; harmless because the world-tile classifier never returns them, but a trap if the mapping is reused.
- Pre-compression classifier 0x140f512c0 has five call sites outside the map generator (0x1403003ff, 0x140300416, 0x1409801b2, 0x140f7b267, 0x140f7b278); it is not exclusive to the initial square count.
- Subregion builder 0x140f52100 is also called from the river generator at 0x140f79889 (normal path), so region/landmass objects are rebuilt at least three times per generation.
- Direct call sites of the classifier 0x140f51940 number 135, not ~50; the UI driver switches its return through a 51-case jump table at 0x140e0079c (cmp eax,0x32) — this switch, not the string cluster, is the binary evidence for the index→name order 0-41.
- [BIN:string 0x1783f14] is an .rdata float (0.25 at VA 0x141785114), not a string; it is absent from strings_ascii.txt and should be tagged as a data constant.
- Param block base is 0x1424f36c8: SUBREGION_MAX at +0x160 (0x1424f3828), GOOD_SQ_COUNTS at +0x3e8/0x3f0/0x3f8 and EVIL_SQ_COUNTS at +0x3ec/0x3f4/0x3fc (interleaved small/medium/large), savagery minimum-count params at +0x1d0..0x1d8 — confirms the report's data-structure note and ties the placer globals to the tokens.
- LAKE and OCEAN tropical variants use the tropical-zone flag only (no latitude blend); the 'tropical iff zone and (blend<66 or deep)' rule applies only to the land biomes.
- Report §5 step 1 lists the destroyed vectors as 'region, landmass' at +0x220/+0x238; the decomp shows +0x220 holds 192-byte landmasses and +0x238 holds 648-byte regions (the Data-structures section has it right).
- Civ placer 0x140f60c30 iterates the landmass vector (world+0x220) at four sites starting at 0x140f61cb1 right after good/evil placement — the natural place to look for the 'not surrounded by ocean' dwarf rule.
- generate_random_interactions also emits weekly WEATHER_FALLING_MATERIAL emissions of creature blood from world.usable_blood_materials (blood rains), not only EVIL_CLOUD/EVIL_RAIN materials.
- evilgood_pic roll: decomp of make-evil/make-good computes (rand31 mod 2^31-1) >> 30, i.e. two values, which does not match the report's 'evil: rand(4)'; unverified either way, worth re-checking.
- The 1-in-10 has_bogeymen roll is a single gate before scanning candidate creatures (line 1503 of decomp/140f918a0.c), then the flag is set if any qualifying creature exists — consistent with the claim but the report's 'when a qualifying creature exists' ordering is reversed (roll first, then scan).
- The two 'Natural savagery' help lines 'They can be found in any wilderness.' / 'They are only found in the untamed wilds.' are referenced at 0x140dfca1a and 0x140dfca88, just beyond the cited range end 0x140dfca17.
- Water biomes ignore the blend: ocean (0x140f51dad) and lake (0x140f51ae6) tropical variants test only the tropical-zone flag, while all land branches need tropical-zone AND (blend<66 OR deep). Consequence: in no-pole worlds (blend=100) land biomes are tropical only at temperature>=85 although oceans/lakes turn tropical at >=75; in pole worlds of <=65 rows land needs scaled lat>=200 vs >170 for water; and in both cases tropical forests with rainfall>=75 are always MOIST broadleaf (DRY needs blend<66, impossible when blend=100).
- Savanna threshold 6: tropical iff lat>170 and (blend<=6 or lat>=200); for savanna rainfall 20-32 the first fully tropical scaled row is 183 (vs 179 with the 66 rule) — i.e. the anomaly delays savanna's tropic line by ~4 rows of 257, the opposite of what the report says.
- The region builder uses two different biome->kind maps: connectivity and landmass membership use 0x140f52000 (no lake kind; lakes=ocean=4, pools->3), while the region object's kind field is set from 0x140f51de0 on the first tile scanned (lakes=5). An inland lake becomes a kind-5 region, a lake touching ocean merges into the ocean region, and a merged region whose lowest-x tile is a lake tile would be recorded as kind 5.
- Lakes are formed before rivers inside the map generator: the lake-bit setters 0x140f53750 (sets byte1|=0x40 at 0x140f54569) and 0x140f530f0 (0x140f5336f) are called at 0x140f4f110/0x140f4f19f, ahead of the river generator call 0x140f4f1d6 and the second region build 0x140f4fb9c; the UI status order ('Running rivers...' before 'Forming lakes') does not reflect this internal order.
- The pre-compression classifier is a selectable mode, not a one-off: helper 0x140f7b1f0 branches on a boolean (test r11b at 0x140f7b262) between 0x140f512c0 x2 and 0x140f50db0+0x140f51940, and 0x140300360 has the same pre/post pattern (0x1403003ff/416 vs 0x140300426/43d); the entity-position function 0x14097bad0 ('CUSTOM_LAW_MAKER','baron') calls the pre-compression variant directly at 0x1409801b2 — worth checking whether that is a stale-scale bug.
- Make-evil's three 33/66 band flags inside the tile loop (0x140f919a7-0x140f919b9) read tile+0x4c, which the classifier uses as salinity (fresh/brackish/salt), not savagery (+0x3e); the report's section 7 'calm/wild/untamed' note is a misread — these flags most likely steer freshwater vs saltwater population choices.
- has_bogeymen ordering: the 1-in-10 roll (rand31 >= 214748365 -> return) happens after the shared finaliser sub_140f5e2d0 and gates a scan of the creature vector 0x1424e6958..0x1424e6960; field_242 is set only if that scan yields at least one candidate.
- Param-block anchors recovered from the parser 0x140de5b10: GOOD_SQ_COUNTS -> +0x3e8/+0x3f0/+0x3f8, EVIL_SQ_COUNTS -> +0x3ec/+0x3f4/+0x3fc, SUBREGION_MAX +0x160, SAVAGERY_RANGES +0x1d0..+0x1d8; the live copy is at 0x1424f36c8 (0x1424f3828 = +0x160, 0x1424f3ab0 = +0x3e8), useful for locating any other parameter global.
- Creature raw flag parser 0x140c45f70 returns 1-based-looking indices (EQUIPMENT_WAGON=1, MUNDANE=2, BIOME_MOUNTAIN=12, OCEAN_ARCTIC=41, SUBTERRANEAN_WATER=42, SUBTERRANEAN_LAVA=44, POOL_TEMPERATE_FRESHWATER=45, LAKE_TROPICAL_SALTWATER=56, RIVER_TROPICAL_SALTWATER=62, SAVAGE=65); a second parser 0x140024340 references the same token list; neither matches the byte-7 bit0/1/2 tests in make-good/make-evil, so those tests are on a different (plant/population) flag array.
- The 'Initial/Region count' helper note: the civ placer's per-landmass tally loop (0x140f61cb1-0x140f61dc5) sizes a local vector to landmasses.size(), increments count[tile.landmass_id] for every candidate point and keeps the argmax — this looks like 'pick the main landmass' logic that the open question on dwarf placement should start from.
- The blend helper's x argument (rdx) is never read, and the classifier passes y*112 in rdx; the helper is effectively (height, y, pole_mode, rainfall).
- The dead rainfall/12 pre-computation (0x140f51988-0x140f519a6, forced to 0 when drainage>=75) exists identically in both classifier variants and is unreachable because the temp<=-5 branch returns at 0x140f51b5b before r9 is consumed.

### Open questions (carried from the report)

- Where is the 'surrounded by the ocean' / island test for dwarf civilisations implemented (region totalboundnum, landmass area, or a local flood in the 43 KB civ placer 0x140f60c30)?
- How does the basic-mode 'Natural savagery' option translate into SAVAGERY_RANGES and savagery min/max (no direct global-address references found)?
- What does the shared post-alignment finaliser 0x140f5e2d0 compute (dead_veg_chance? population bookkeeping?)
- Is the savanna blend threshold of 6 (vs 66 elsewhere) a bug in the original source or intentional?
- Do the post-erosion 'altitude locations' checks and erosion itself operate on the compressed (≥150 mountain) elevation scale, as the code order suggests?
- Which world_region fields hold the population vector and the name, and how pools/rivers (biome indices 30–35, 42–47) are assigned at local-map level.

---

## geology_underground

**Title:** Geology and the underground: stone layers, minerals, soil, aquifers, caverns, caves, volcanoes, magma sea, underworld  
**Report:** analyst report `geology_underground.md` (session working file, not committed; the ledger is the adjudicated record)  

**Summary:** DF 53.16 geology is a two-level model: ~W*H/100 geo-biomes (each a stack of soil + ~10 stone layers with mineral inclusion lists) are seeded at random tiles, grown by an anisotropic seeded flood over same-kind tiles (kind = land/shallow ocean/deep ocean/sand desert, x volcanic when volcanism>=90), reseeded per region, and every world tile stores only an int16 geo_index. Layer stones are chosen least-used-first; minerals are placed per inorganic per frame with rand(MINERAL_SCARCITY) < ENVIRONMENT frequency and a minimum-one-per-batch guarantee. Caverns/magma sea/underworld are feature layers (up to 5) tiled into regions by a second flood, each region owning a feature_init with biased-low openness/passage/water clamped to params; first-layer depth = min 5x5 elevation - LEVELS_ABOVE_LAYER_1. Caves are world sites of type 2 placed from candidate lists; volcanoes are tiles with volcanism==100. Embark labels derive from the layer stack; heavy aquifer iff drainage % 20 == 7.

### Refuted

- **`geology_underground-07`** (analyst status *verified*, confidence 0.75): Layer recipes are selected by kind via jump tables: land kinds get four 1-z SOIL layers, ocean kinds four SOIL_OCEAN layers, sand desert one SOIL_SAND layer of thickness 1-3; the stone stack starts with four layers of the kind's primary sedimentary class then alternates 50/50 between deeper classes until 10 stone layers; thickness per stone layer is a small random integer (rand(5)+2 in the default igneous-intrusive case).
  - Evidence: [BIN:func 0x140f8d9f0] (disassembly 0x140f8da42-0x140f8dd94; angr decomp of default case)
  - Verdict: downgrade: Disassembly 0x140f8da20-0x140f8df94 and the real jump tables (0x140f8df98, 0x140f8dfb8) show: kinds 0-1 four 1-z SOIL, kinds 2-3 four SOIL_OCEAN, but kinds 4-7 (shallow ocean AND sand desert) one SOIL_SAND layer of thickness 1-3; stone stack = 1 explicit primary + loop of 10 (first 4 primary, then 6 x 50/50 METAMORPHIC vs IGNEOUS_INTRUSIVE via cmp 0x40000000) + final IGNEOUS_INTRUSIVE = 12 stone layers (volcanic kinds: IGNEOUS_EXTRUSIVE then 10 x uniform{META,EXTR,INTR} + final INTR); every stone layer uses bottom = top-2-rand(5), not just a default case.
  - Verdict: refuted: Jump tables at 0x140f8df98/0x140f8dfb8 show: soil = kinds 0,1 -> 4x SOIL, kinds 2,3 -> 4x SOIL_OCEAN, kinds 4-7 (shallow ocean AND sand desert) -> one SOIL_SAND with bottom -1..-3; stone = 1 pre-loop + 4 in-loop primary layers (5, not 4), then 6 rolled 50/50 METAMORPHIC vs IGNEOUS_INTRUSIVE, then an unconditional final IGNEOUS_INTRUSIVE at 0x140f8df29 (12 stone layers, not 10); volcanic kinds 1,3,5,7 use 1 IGNEOUS_EXTRUSIVE + 10 uniform 3-way {META,EXTR,INTR} + final INTRUSIVE; every stone layer has top-bottom = rand(5)+2 (3-7 levels by the game's own depth accounting, vs 1-level soil).
- **`geology_underground-17`** (analyst status *verified*, confidence 0.80): Each feature layer is tiled into contiguous regions by a seeded flood (one jittered seed per tile, random priority 0-3); each region gets a 280-byte feature_init object whose water = WATER_MIN + rand(range) and whose openness and passage-density min/max are random percentages biased toward low values and clamped to the parameter ranges.
  - Evidence: [BIN:func 0x140f8a020] (decomp lines 715-935; mirrors 0x1424f3830-0x1424f3844) [BIN:func 0x140efb360]
  - Verdict: downgrade: 280-byte alloc + 0x140efb360, water = WATER_MIN + rand(MAX-MIN+1), clamping and min/max ordering are confirmed, and passage density is biased low (p=9/10 -> 0..19 if PASSAGE_MIN<=20, p=3/4 -> 0..40 if <=40), but openness is biased HIGH: 0x140f8aff0 'add dx,0x3c' gives 60..100 with p=3/4 when OPENNESS_MAX>=60 and 0x140f8b0d3 'add r8w,0x50' gives 80..99 with p=19/20 when >=80, contradicting the report's '3/4 chance of a 0-9 roll'.
  - Verdict: refuted: 280-byte feature_init, water = WATER_MIN + rand(MAX-MIN+1), +140 = rand(100), seeds jittered by rand(9)-4 per axis with priority rand(4) are confirmed, but the bias direction is half wrong: openness min/max are biased HIGH (0x140f8afb1-0x140f8aff0: 75% -> 60+rand(41) when OPENNESS_MAX>=60; 95% -> 80+rand(21) when >=80) and only passage density is biased low (90% -> rand(21) when PASSAGE_MIN<=20; 75% -> rand(41) when <=40), before clamping and ordering.
- **`geology_underground-24`** (analyst status *public*, confidence 0.70): The basic-settings 'Mineral occurrence' field offers the labels Everywhere / Frequent / Sparse / Rare / Very rare, which set MINERAL_SCARCITY (public values approx. 100 / 2500 / 10000 / 50000 / 100000).
  - Evidence: [BIN:string 0x1718960] [BIN:func 0x140df39f0 @0x140dfd7e7-0x140dfd95f] [BIN:string 0x1718a38] [BIN:string 0x1718a48] [BIN:string 0x16a8cc4] [BIN:string 0x1718a54] [BIN:string 0x17189e0] [PUBLIC] wiki values
  - Verdict: confirmed: Reproduced: 'Mineral occurrence' (0x1718960) referenced at 0x140dfcb39 and Everywhere/Frequent/Sparse/Rare/Very rare at 0x140dfd7e7/0x140dfd84b/0x140dfd893/0x140dfd8d8/0x140dfd91d, all inside 0x140df39f0; the numeric MINERAL_SCARCITY values are correctly labelled public.
  - Verdict: refuted: Labels exist and are drawn for option index 0..4 as Very rare, Rare, Sparse, Frequent, Everywhere (0x140dfd7bb switch, selection field viewscreen+0x264), but the stage worker's preset writer at 0x140df0519-0x140df0560 stores 50000, 10000, 2500, 500 (r9d=0x1f4), 100 into the param field ([rbp+0x328] = MINERAL_SCARCITY +0x388), so Very rare=50000, Rare=10000, Sparse=2500, Frequent=500, Everywhere=100 rather than the public 100000/50000/10000/2500/100.

### Skeptic extra findings

- Shallow-ocean geo-biome kinds (4,5) use the sand-desert soil recipe (one SOIL_SAND layer of thickness 1-3); only deep-ocean kinds (2,3) get four SOIL_OCEAN layers (jump table 0x140f8df98).
- Stone stack per kind is 12 layers, not 10: 1 explicit primary + loop of 10 (4 primary then 6 random) + a final IGNEOUS_INTRUSIVE; non-volcanic kinds alternate METAMORPHIC/IGNEOUS_INTRUSIVE 50/50, volcanic kinds start with IGNEOUS_EXTRUSIVE and draw 10 layers uniformly from {METAMORPHIC, IGNEOUS_EXTRUSIVE, IGNEOUS_INTRUSIVE} (jump table 0x140f8dfb8).
- Feature-init openness values are biased toward HIGH percentages (60-100 with p=3/4, 80-99 with p=19/20 when the max allows), passage density toward low; the report states the opposite for openness.
- With CAVERN_LAYER_COUNT=2 the displayed cavern numbers are 1 and 3; the second int16 (+134) holds the 1-based ordinal (1,2) while +136 holds the displayed number.
- The cavern builder 0x140f8a020 consumes LEVELS_ABOVE_LAYER_2..5 and LEVELS_AT_BOTTOM at 0x140f8b586-0x140f8b5a6 (cumulative subtraction stored at region_map_entry+0), answering the report's open question about where they enter; only LEVELS_ABOVE_GROUND is deferred to local-map code.
- Geo-biome growth runs exactly two passes (seed+grow, reseed-per-region+grow), not 'until coverage is complete'; tiles of a different kind inside an already-reseeded region can remain unassigned.
- Mineral pass order is CLUSTER(2), VEIN(1), CLUSTER_SMALL(3), CLUSTER_ONE(4); the later passes also run nested ENVIRONMENT_SPEC placement into inclusions already present (mode 1-3 calls), and 0x140d93290 returns 0 when the candidate is the layer's own material.
- Magma layer (index 3) is built when HAVE_BOTTOM_LAYER_1 OR HAVE_BOTTOM_LAYER_2 is set, matching the UI label 'Bottom Layer (Forces Magma)'.
- Inorganic flag bit 2 is set only alongside SEDIMENTARY, METAMORPHIC, SOIL and SOIL_SAND, not with ocean sedimentary, igneous or SOIL_OCEAN classes.
- FIRED_MAT is a MATERIAL_REACTION_PRODUCT key in the raws (clay -> CERAMIC_EARTHENWARE/STONEWARE) and the embark screen looks it up through helper 0x1414566b0 on list +0x350, distinct from the +0x650 REACTION_CLASS list used for FLUX.
- Report summary and algorithm sketch say a volcano has volcanism '== 100' / 'equals 100' while the binary (and claim 21) use >= 100.
- Vanilla ENVIRONMENT placement count is 146, not 156.
- Cited strings 0x173f930/0x173f948 are world_gen_param tokens referenced from 0x140f82610 (likely a param reader), not the cave rejection messages.
- Soil layers are stored with top==bottom (one level), so a stone layer with bottom = top-2-rand(5) spans rand(5)+3 levels inclusive; the 'rand(5)+2' figure is the offset, not the inclusive level count.
- Tile attribute getter 0x1403d42c0 has no .pdata/unwind entry (leaf function), so func.py cannot bound it; the code itself is verified.
- Runtime order in map-generator state 6 (0x140f4f1db-0x140f502d0): mountain-slope 0x140f90aa0 -> ... -> param writer 0x140f80330 -> 0x140f89b50/0x140f89900 -> geology+mineral builder looped in ~50 ms slices (cmp rax,0x32 after 0x140fb36f0) across frames until progress 0x1424e5c10 >= max(4*N_inorganics,500) -> cavern builder 0x140f8a020 -> state 7. The report has caverns before geology.
- Corrected layer recipe (0x140f8d9f0, tables 0x140f8df98/0x140f8dfb8): soil by kind: 0,1 -> 4x SOIL (top==bottom, heights 0,-1,-2,-3); 2,3 -> 4x SOIL_OCEAN; 4,5,6,7 -> 1x SOIL_SAND with bottom -(rand(3)+1). Stone: kinds 0,6 -> SEDIMENTARY; 2 -> SEDIMENTARY_OCEAN_DEEP; 4 -> SEDIMENTARY_OCEAN_SHALLOW: 5 primary layers (1 before the loop + 4 in it), 6 layers rolled 50/50 IGNEOUS_INTRUSIVE (r31<2^30) vs METAMORPHIC, then one final IGNEOUS_INTRUSIVE; kinds 1,3,5,7 (volcanic) -> 1 IGNEOUS_EXTRUSIVE + 10 layers uniform rand(3) in {METAMORPHIC, IGNEOUS_EXTRUSIVE, IGNEOUS_INTRUSIVE} + final IGNEOUS_INTRUSIVE. Every stone layer: bottom = top - 2 - rand(5). Totals: 16 layers (4 soil + 12 stone) for land/deep ocean, 13 for sand desert/shallow ocean; the report's '10-15 layers' is wrong.
- Shallow-ocean geo-biomes (kinds 4,5: ocean biome with elevation >= 50) get a single SOIL_SAND cap exactly like sand deserts, not SOIL_OCEAN; only deep-ocean kinds (2,3) get SOIL_OCEAN.
- world_geo_layer vein vectors: +0x08 vector<int32> material; +0x20 vector<int16> nested-in index (0xffff for direct placements, parent vein index for nested ones); +0x38 vector<int8> inclusion type (1 VEIN, 2 CLUSTER, 3 CLUSTER_SMALL, 4 CLUSTER_ONE); +0x50 vector<int8> depth code (bottom - biome base + 60 + 1, clamped >= 0). This also explains the embark ore list accepting 'codes 1 or 2': it lists ores present as VEIN or CLUSTER only.
- Mineral placement pass structure per (inorganic, pass) call: CLUSTER first, then VEIN, CLUSTER_SMALL, CLUSTER_ONE. Each pass has a direct sub-pass (matcher mode 0: ENVIRONMENT class by layer type, then ENVIRONMENT_SPEC with host == layer material) and a nested sub-pass (matcher mode = parent vein's inclusion type 1..3, host == parent vein material, ENVIRONMENT_SPEC only; mode 4 returns 0, so nothing nests in CLUSTER_ONE). The matcher also returns 0 when the candidate is the layer material itself.
- ENVIRONMENT matcher table (0x140d93438): layer type 0 SOIL <- class 0; 5 SOIL_OCEAN <- 1; 6 SOIL_SAND <- 2; 2 METAMORPHIC <- 3; 1 SEDIMENTARY, 7 SEDIMENTARY_OCEAN_SHALLOW and 8 SEDIMENTARY_OCEAN_DEEP <- class 4 (SEDIMENTARY) or 7 (ALLUVIAL); 4 IGNEOUS_INTRUSIVE <- 5; 3 IGNEOUS_EXTRUSIVE <- 6. ALL_STONE / IGNEOUS_ALL have no case here, so they are presumably expanded into several class entries by the parser (not verified).
- Tail-batch rule: after the full 100-candidate batches, the remaining candidates are rolled (repeat-until-placed) only if nothing has been placed yet in this call; otherwise they are discarded (0x140f8c676-0x140f8c684 and the equivalent checks in the other passes). The practical guarantee is therefore 'each inorganic that has any candidate layer gets at least one inclusion per inclusion-type pass'.
- Geo-biome growth runs exactly two passes (cmp di,2 at 0x140f8c344): pass 0 random seeds + flood, pass 1 one reseed per still-uncovered region id + flood. Because growth only crosses same-kind tiles and each side roll can fail, tiles can remain unassigned (geo_index -1) after pass 1; the report's 'iterates until coverage is complete' is unsupported. The 3rd and 4th seed values are carried along but not used in the growth tests.
- Mineral occurrence presets (stage worker 0x140df0519-0x140df0560, writing MINERAL_SCARCITY via [rbp+0x328] = param+0x388): option 0 'Very rare' = 50000, 1 'Rare' = 10000, 2 'Sparse' = 2500 (the classic advanced default), 3 'Frequent' = 500, 4 'Everywhere' = 100. The same stack frame also sets +0x32c/+0x330/+0x334 (MEGABEAST_CAP/SEMIMEGABEAST_CAP/TITAN_NUMBER) from the neighbouring basic-settings option.
- Cavern feature_init randomisation (0x140f8afa3-0x140f8b31b): openness min = 60+rand(41) with 75% probability when OPENNESS_MAX >= 60 else rand(100); openness max = 80+rand(21) with 95% probability when OPENNESS_MAX >= 80 else rand(100); passage-density min = rand(21) with 90% probability when PASSAGE_DENSITY_MIN <= 20 else rand(100); passage max = rand(41) with 75% probability when PASSAGE_DENSITY_MIN <= 40 else rand(100); all clamped to the parameter range and ordered min <= max; +140 = rand(100). Caverns are therefore mostly open with sparse passages, the opposite of the report's 'biased toward low values' for openness.
- Cavern layer numbering (0x140f8a9a1-0x140f8a9fc): field +0x86 = ordinal+1 always; field +0x88 = 3 for the single cavern when CAVERN_LAYER_COUNT==1, = 1 and 3 for two caverns, = 1,2,3 for three.
- Cave candidates: world tiles with elevation >= 150 that pass 0x140f53010 (border/adjacency), have tile-flag bit 3 (bitfield at entry+0x20) clear and pass 0x140faf090; cave site size = CAVE_MIN_SIZE + rand(max(CAVE_MIN,CAVE_MAX) - CAVE_MIN + 1) (>= 1), plus two int32 fields +0x230/+0x234 = 110 - rand(21).
- Thickness convention: the embark readout accumulates depth as (bottom - top - 1) per layer, so a soil layer with top==bottom counts as 1 level, the SOIL_SAND cap 2-4 levels, and each stone layer rand(5)+3 levels; the report's 'rand(5)+2' and 'sand 1-3' mix top-bottom differences with inclusive level counts.
- The RNG wrapper 0x140eb1820 is a multi-stream MT19937: an int16 stream index at 0x142152f70 selects one of several 0x9c0-byte buffers at 0x142288880 (index array 0x14212ba18) and the twist is inlined (SSE for the first 224 words), separate from the 0x1405xxxxx wrapper family named in the brief.
- Biome-type function 0x140f51940 (decomp available) returns 24/25/26 for deserts by drainage (>=66 badland, 33-65 rock, <33 sand), 27/28/29 for oceans by temperature, i.e. the geo-biome 'kind' thresholds are reachable from in-binary evidence rather than the public enum alone.
- ENVIRONMENT tokens occur only in inorganic_stone_gem.txt and inorganic_stone_mineral.txt (146 total, 4 fields each); the report's '156' is a typo relative to its own breakdown.
- The per-tile embark readout routine (0x141045214-0x141045cc0) can never set both aquifer flags (one tile, one drainage value); a second readout routine starting near 0x141048fa7 (also using the %20 idiom at 0x14104937d and a FLUX check) exists and is the likely home of the embark-rectangle 'Varied aquifer' aggregation.

### Open questions (carried from the report)

- Exact per-kind stone recipes for the volcanic kinds (extrusive vs intrusive alternation) and per-class thickness ranges beyond the one case read in 0x140f8d9f0.
- Meaning of the two int8 vein vectors (layer+0x38 placement/nesting code with values 1/2; layer+0x50 depth code) and of the 0-100 percentage stored at feature_init+140.
- The elevation threshold and adjacency test (0x140f53010) defining 'border mountain squares' and the mid-elevation band for low-lying caves.
- How per-tile feature-layer elevations (region_map_entry+0/+4) become cavern z-ranges at embark (0x140fe9990 family) and where LEVELS_AT_BOTTOM / LEVELS_ABOVE_LAYER_2..5 enter.
- Which code constructs per-site feature_init_deep_special_tubest / deep_surface_portalst / magma_poolst objects and the magma pool/pipe placement rules.
- Mapping of the 'Subterranean Water/Chasm/Lava' biome names to feature-layer regions.
- Numeric MINERAL_SCARCITY values applied by the five Mineral-occurrence labels (preset application code not located).

## vegetation_wildlife

**Title:** Vegetation field, plant distribution, wildlife and beast populations  
**Report:** analyst report `vegetation_wildlife.md` (session working file, not committed; the ledger is the adjudicated record)  

**Summary:** Vegetation in DF 53.16 is a derived per-tile value (helper 0x140f772a0: above freezing = rainfall; freezing = rainfall/6, or 0 when drainage >= 75), computed in stage 3 and recomputed in stage 6 after orographic rain (the "Growing vegetation..." stage). "Importing wildlife..." is one 7.7 KB function (0x140f5c390) that intersects raw BIOME flags with each region's biome set, weights by FREQUENCY x world-size factor, gates SAVAGE creatures on untamed tiles, caps animal kinds per region at min(50, 7 + tiles/10) (the 53.16 release-note change), and appends population records via 0x140f95200. A 43 KB prehistory function (0x140f60c30) places caves, MEGABEAST_CAP megabeasts, TITAN_NUMBER titans, civs (rejecting farming civs with no orchard/field crops) and SEMIMEGABEAST_CAP semimegabeasts. The real-world-extinct option rewrites creature flags at raw finalisation (0x1406c5b30). Raw tables for 225 plants, 767 creatures and 200 extinct creatures are included.
