### Refuted

- **`vegetation_wildlife-01`** (analyst status *verified*, confidence 0.97): Per-tile vegetation is derived by helper 0x140f772a0: if temperature > -5 it equals rainfall; otherwise rainfall/6, or 0 when drainage >= 75 (tile stride 112, fields +0x36/+0x32/+0x3a).
  - Evidence: [BIN:func 0x140f772a0-0x140f772e7] cmp word [r8+0x36],0xfffb; magic 0x2aaaaaab divide by 6; cmp word [r8+0x3a],0x4b
  - Verdict: refuted: Disassembly at 0x140f772a0-0x140f772e7 confirms stride 0x70, fields +0x36/+0x32/+0x3a, thresholds -5 and 75, but the cold branch is imul 0x2aaaaaab then sar edx,1 (plus sign fix), which is signed division by 12, not 6 (simulated: 60->5, 24->2); the same /6 error is repeated in elevation_fields.md.
  - Verdict: confirmed: Disassembly of the 72-byte leaf at 0x140f772a0 (no .pdata entry) matches exactly: temp(+0x36) > -5 returns rain(+0x32); else rain/6 via 0x2aaaaaab/sar 1, returning 0 when drainage(+0x3a) >= 75; stride 0x70 through world+0x2b0.
- **`vegetation_wildlife-09`** (analyst status *inferred*, confidence 0.75): Population records are typed 0 animal, 1 vermin, 3 innumerable (VERMIN_FISH), 4 colony/eater (VERMIN_EATER flag), 5 tree, 6 grass, 7 bush, matching the public world_population_type order; animal is chosen for LARGE_ROAMING or HAS_ANY_LARGE_PREDATOR creatures.
  - Evidence: decomp/140f5c390.c lines 1161-1217 (types 5/6/7), 1469-1498 (types 4/3/0/1); [PUBLIC] df-structures world_population_type
  - Verdict: confirmed: decomp 1161-1217 call sub_140f95200 with 5/6/7 for plants; 1469-1498 use 4 when byte0 bit7 (VERMIN_EATER), 3 when byte0 bit6 (VERMIN_FISH), 0 when byte9&4 (bit 74 HAS_ANY_LARGE_PREDATOR) or byte1&1 (bit 8 LARGE_ROAMING), else 1; status 'inferred' is appropriate since the type names come from public df-structures order.
  - Verdict: refuted: Type codes 0/1/3/4/5/6/7 and the LARGE_ROAMING(8)/HAS_ANY_LARGE_PREDATOR(74) -> animal rule hold, but the parser (0x1406bdf00) assigns VERMIN_EATER=3, VERMIN_GROUNDER=4, VERMIN_ROTTER=5, VERMIN_SOIL=6, VERMIN_SOIL_COLONY=7, VERMIN_FISH=9, LOOSE_CLUSTERS=10, so the byte-0 sign test giving type 4 is VERMIN_SOIL_COLONY (ant/termite/bee = ColonyInsect) and the bit-6 test giving type 3 is VERMIN_SOIL, while VERMIN_FISH creatures fall to type 1; plant side holds (GRASS -> bit 79 at 0x140d8baa9, TREE -> 78).
- **`vegetation_wildlife-10`** (analyst status *verified*, confidence 0.80): After weighted placement the importer tops every region up to 7 kinds each of tree, grass and bush and to the region cap of each animal category by random raw picks (0xffff sentinel) using the world-gen MT wrapper 0x140eb1820.
  - Evidence: decomp/140f5c390.c lines 1238-1290 (iter < 7), 1608-1675 (< v7); [BIN:func 0x140eb1820-0x140eb1a03] MT state stride 0x9c0, seed-type index 0x142152f70
  - Verdict: refuted: Lines 1238-1290 and 1608-1675 contain six 'iter < 7' loops and five '< v7' loops with the 0xffff sentinel, but they iterate fixed 7-slot tracker arrays (v82.., v97..) that are filled during the weighted placement loops (lines 1101, 1508) and contain no RNG call; the only four sub_140eb1820 calls in the function are at lines 604/616/684/695 in the candidate passes (rand % world_w, rand % world_h per raw), so the top-up is not 'random raw picks using the MT wrapper' (the MT wrapper facts 0x9c0 stride and 0x142152f70 index are correct).
  - Verdict: refuted: The top-up lists (six 7-slot plant lists, five cap-sized creature lists) are filled in the placement loop by insertion-sorting unplaced raws by distance between the raw's home point and the region centre (decomp 1095-1150, 1555-1590) and consumed in that order with no RNG; the only four MT calls (0x140f5c668-0x140f5c8f4) draw each raw's random home x/y in [0,w)x[0,h) during the candidate passes.
- **`vegetation_wildlife-25`** (analyst status *public*, confidence 0.70): Public df-structures layout: a world_population record holds type, race/plant index, count_min/count_max and owner region; DF stores the POPULATION_NUMBER range rather than rolling a single count at world generation.
  - Evidence: [PUBLIC] DFHack df-structures world_population; consistent with the helper's error strings but the min/max store itself was not traced
  - Verdict: confirmed: Labelled [PUBLIC] in the report and the claim; the df-structures world_population layout (type, race/plant, count_min/count_max, owner region) is well-known public knowledge and the report states the min/max store was not traced.
  - Verdict: refuted: The helper rolls one number: 0x140a45ed0 draws uniform in [POPULATION_NUMBER min (+0x12e), max (+0x12c)] with the world-gen MT wrapper, multiplies by the eligible square count, raises it to a CLUSTER_NUMBER-range roll x squares if lower (0x140f95b17-0x140f95b47), floors at squares, caps at 10,000,000 and writes the same value to both count fields (+4 and +8) of the 0x28-byte record (type +0, raw +2, three -1 fields), so no range is stored.

### Skeptic extra findings

- Cold-tile vegetation divisor is 12, not 6: 0x140f772c9-0x140f772d7 is imul 0x2aaaaaab; sar edx,1; sign fix (MSVC idiom for x/12). elevation_fields.md lines 48 and 116 repeat the /6 error.
- The four MT-wrapper (0x140eb1820) draws in the importer are rand % world_w (world+0xa0) and rand % world_h (world+0xa4) per candidate raw (decomp 598-620, 684-695), i.e. a random per-raw start coordinate; this answers the report's open questions about the 'two small integer parameters <= 1' and the cursor start.
- Top-up loops (decomp 1238-1290, 1608-1675) iterate 7-slot tracker arrays (v82/v85/v88/v91/v94 for plants, v97 for creatures) filled during the weighted placement loops (lines 1101, 1508); they contain no RNG call, so the 'minimum diversity top-up' is deterministic from the trackers.
- The importer's candidate filter (decomp 503-562) also skips OCCURS_AS_ENTITY_RACE (byte9&2), EVIL (byte8&1 = bit 64) and GOOD (byte7 sign = bit 63) creatures, not only boss/non-existent ones; good/evil populations come from the make-good/make-evil rebuilds.
- The final fill pass under REAL_WORLD_EXTINCT==1 (0x140f5de27-0x140f5de47, decomp 1711) has no small-landmass or <=5-tile exemption: every HAS_REAL_WORLD_EXTINCT raw is skipped there.
- 0x1424e6938 is the creature-raws struct whose +0x20 vector is 0x1424e6958 (0x1400e3550 bounds-checks dx against that vector and tests flag bit r8d <= 0x87 on raw->+0x1a8); it is not a world-gen 'candidate list' as section 9 says. 0x1406ce110 maps a biome id to a flag bit and 0x140d92e90 is called once before the region loop; neither is a 'random-pick helper'.
- 'Growing vegetation...' and 'Verifying terrain...' share the same gate (world+0x268 non-empty and 0x1424e5c10 >= 0x1424e5c14, the progress counter of 0x140f8b7c0 called from the block-6/7 slice path at 0x140f50294, with total set at 0x140df0a82 as max(4*x, 500)), so they appear together only after that sliced pass finishes; the stage word is advanced at the end of each generator call (0x140f506eb inc, 0x140f506ef store), with self-increments in block 4 (0x140f4effe) and block 9 (0x140f5058c).
- The embark Trees value is produced by 0x140f77260: 0 when temperature <= -5 or vegetation/rainfall <= 0, otherwise vegetation (computed as veg*rain/rain); a value <= 0 skips the 'Trees:' line entirely.
- In the BEAST_END_YEAR check, 0x1424f3a34 is END_YEAR (+0x36c per params_rejection.md), not part of the BEAST_END_YEAR year/percentage block as section 11 states; the test is year >= BEAST_END_YEAR.year and year < END_YEAR.
- Section 5's wording 'for each biome id of the region, the plant's BIOME flag bit for that id must be set or the raw is skipped' is misleading: a mismatched id only continues to the next id (goto LABEL_140f5cf06 at the loop end), so the test is any-match, as claim 07 states.
- Block 7 (0x140f50239-0x140f5040e) counts tiles with elevation >= 150 (r14d=0x96) and emits 'Not enough low/medium/high altitude locations after erosion', which fits 'Verifying terrain...' better than block 6 does; block 6 holds Mountain-Slope 0x140f90aa0, wind 0x140655f00, the vegetation recompute, 0x140f52100 and 0x140f8a020.
- The crop-rejection path returns al=1 (mov al,1 at 0x140f6abc5 before jmp to the epilogue at 0x140f6b529), so the placer signals rejection with a true return.
- tokmap.py output starts at index 27 and lists only 35 of the biome names (SUBTERRANEAN_WATER/CHASM/LAVA at 43-45, before the POOL/LAKE/RIVER entries), so the report's early indices (EQUIPMENT_WAGON 1 ... BIOME_MOUNTAIN 11) are from public order, not extracted values; the biome block spans 11-63 (53 slots) in this binary versus 51 in public df-structures.
- Engine creature-flag layout differs from df-structures by two extra pre-biome flags: bit 0 'EQUIPMENT' (also set by the EQUIPMENT_WAGON token together with bit 1) and an unnamed bit 11; BIOME_MOUNTAIN is bit 12 and BIOME_RIVER_TROPICAL_SALTWATER bit 62, so any flag number taken from public df-structures must be shifted by +2 for biomes and by +1 before them.
- The importer's per-raw 'cursor start coordinate' (open question) is a random world position drawn once per raw: the two 'small integer parameters' are world width/height (world+0xa0/+0xa4) and the two MT draws give x in [0,w), y in [0,h); a raw is placed in a region when that home point, pulled toward the region centre by reach = scale*FREQUENCY/100 (clamped at the centre), lands inside the region's bounding box; the distance trackers keep each raw's nearest region for the final sweep.
- Population count formula (open question): count = roll[POPULATION_NUMBER] x eligible squares, raised to roll[CLUSTER_NUMBER] x squares if smaller, floored at squares, capped at 10,000,000, stored in both count fields; squares = sum of the region biome histogram over the raw's BIOME bits.
- Good and evil creatures (bits 63/64) and good/evil plants (plant bits 56/57) are excluded from the wildlife importer and are placed only by the make-good/make-evil region rebuilds; savage plants (plant bit 58) obey the same untamed-region rule as SAVAGE creatures.
- Entity raw flags gating the crop rejection: 51 INDOOR_ORCHARDS, 52 OUTDOOR_ORCHARDS (orchard pass), 18 INDOOR_FARMING, 19 OUTDOOR_FARMING (non-orchard pass); the check runs once over the global entity vector after all entities are founded.
- Finaliser value 4 rewrites caste PET_EXOTIC(55)->PET(54) and MOUNT_EXOTIC(101)->MOUNT(100), per the caste parser at 0x1406eb7fe/0x1406eb85e/0x1406e317e.
- Caste boss bits from the caste parser: MEGABEAST 110, SEMIMEGABEAST 111, FEATURE_BEAST 131, TITAN 132, UNIQUE_DEMON 133/134, LARGE_PREDATOR 20, BENIGN 34, COMMON_DOMESTIC 16; plant bits: SPRING..WINTER 0-3, GOOD 56, EVIL 57, SAVAGE 58, TREE 78, GRASS 79.
- Stage 6 structure: first tick runs the terrain pass (sweep, slope, temperature lapse, vegetation, salinity, second region build, SUBREGION_MAX), later ticks run mineral slices through 0x140f8b7c0 until 0x1424e5c10 >= 0x1424e5c14, then 0x140f8a020; stage 7 is the 'Not enough low/medium/high altitude locations after erosion' check (rejections 32-34); so by content the labels 'Growing vegetation...' / 'Verifying terrain...' / 'Importing wildlife...' name blocks 6/7/8.
- The report's §7 low-bit entries (MUNDANE 2, LARGE_ROAMING 9, LOOSE_CLUSTERS 10, BIOME_MOUNTAIN 11, vermin 3-7) are wrong and its §5 candidate-pass flag names inherit the error; the tested bits themselves (3-9 and 8) are correct and correspond to VERMIN_EATER/GROUNDER/ROTTER/SOIL/SOIL_COLONY/LARGE_ROAMING/VERMIN_FISH; LOOSE_CLUSTERS (10) is not a candidate gate.
- Embark 'Trees:' reads vegetation through getter 0x140f77260, which returns 0 for freezing tiles (temp <= -5) regardless of the stored rain/6 value, so cold tiles never show a Trees label.

### Open questions (carried from the report)

- Exact semantics of the importer's per-raw cursor start coordinate and the six -1/0xffff 'nearest region' trackers (lines 940-1070 of decomp/140f5c390.c).
- Which two parameters (compared with <= 1) gate the random draws in the creature candidate pass.
- Plant flag bit names (bits 20/21, 56/57, 78/79) were not recovered from an engine table; only creature flags were.
- How POPULATION_NUMBER/CLUSTER_NUMBER become count_min/count_max and whether the region square count scales them (helper 0x140f95200 arithmetic obscured by string building).
- 'Making cave pops...' (cavern populations by UNDERGROUND_DEPTH) and the second stage-8 callee 0x140f74cb0 were not traced.
- Meaning of the entity crop-entry flag masks 0x73/0x80 and the structure of the entity resource container at +0x1c68.
- Role of 0x140bdfd10 (20 KB), the other reader of MEGABEAST_CAP/SEMIMEGABEAST_CAP (possibly in-history beast spawning).
- Names of caste flags 54/55 and 100/101 rewritten for the 'pets and work animals' option.

---

## gap_post_map_pipeline_order

**Title:** Actual execution order of the post-map phases (stage 9+): prehistory placer, year loop, finalisation  
**Report:** analyst report `gap_post_map_pipeline_order.md` (session working file, not committed; the ledger is the adjudicated record)  

**Summary:** Stage 9 of the map generator is one re-entrant function (0x140f6b660) driven in a 100 ms GetTickCount time slice per frame. With year==1 it runs prehistory init (stepper reset, nine final biome checks → rejection code 2, vector reserves) and then one placer phase per call (caves → good/evil → megabeasts → other beasts → cave pops → cave civs → civilizations), each phase setting the next byte 0x1424e6198..9f; the UI label names the running phase (no off-by-one). Afterwards a 17-sub-step year machine (counter 0x1424e6648) runs; sub-step 13 increments the year, sub-step 16 recomputes ages, snapshots every 10th year and evaluates END_YEAR / BEAST_END_YEAR percentage / 'Use world now'. The finish flag 0x1426388e8 switches the generator to one-element-per-frame finalisation (civ mats, art, uniforms, sites), eight post-passes, an RNG pop and stage 10. 'Recounting legends...' is a static stage 8–9 line. HISTORY_SEED is pushed at megabeast placement and popped only after finalisation.

### Refuted

- **`gap_post_map_pipeline_order-05`** (analyst status *verified*, confidence 0.93): The placer 0x140f60c30 runs exactly one phase per call and sets a byte on completion: caves→0x6199 (0x140f61c64), good/evil→0x619a (0x140f6238b), megabeasts→0x619b (0x140f62a47), other beasts→0x619c (0x140f6624a), cave pops→0x619d (0x140f6636c), cave civs→0x619e (0x140f66540), civilizations→0x619f (0x140f6b514).
  - Evidence: [BIN:func 0x140f60c6f, 0x140f61c83, 0x140f623b2, 0x140f62a5a, 0x140f662a3, 0x140f66378] tests; strings 'Not enough mountain cave locations' 0x140f61046, 'Not enough entity placement locations' 0x140f66f7d, 'Placed farming entity without orchard crops' 0x140f6ab91
  - Verdict: refuted: All seven byte tests/sets and the three strings reproduce at the stated addresses, but 'exactly one phase per call' is contradicted: after F sets 0x619e at 0x140f66540 execution falls straight into G's initialisation with no return, and G returns false repeatedly (0x140f67259 right after storing the civ count, 0x140f6a80d after a placement) and is re-entered at 0x140f66f32 via the 0x619e test and a '0x1424e6224 > 0' check, so civilization placement spans many stepper calls.

### Skeptic extra findings

- Section 2.1/2.2 error: the reserve helper 0x140b501a0 does 'add rcx,0x60' before its capacity check, so prehistory init reserves the vector at 0x1424f3ba8+0x60 = 0x1424f3c08 (historical figures) to TOTAL_CIV_POPULATION, not the events vector; this strengthens the figures identification in section 4.
- Placer phase G is re-entrant across stepper calls: it stores the civ target into 0x1424e6224 and returns at 0x140f67259, is re-entered at 0x140f66f32 (0x619e set) while 0x1424e6224 > 0, decrements the counter per placement (0x140f67456) and returns at 0x140f6a80d; phase F falls through into G's init at 0x140f66547 without returning. The 'one phase per call' statements in sections 1, 2.3, 5 and 7 need rewording, and the UI's 'Placing civilizations... (' value is civs remaining, not placed.
- RNG stream stack: the buffer stack holds 10 levels (0x140777ea0 increments the depth word, logs 'Random Buffer Overload' and clamps the mirror 0x142152f70 to 9 when depth >= 10; buffer array 0x142288880 with stride 0x9c0); the 19 limit in 0x1407780a0 is only the per-level tag array size.
- NAME_SEED at param +0x60 is verified, not inferred: the writer pairs lea rdi,[rbp+0x60] (0x140f804f4) with 'NAME_SEED:' (0x173ec88); likewise the BEAST_END_YEAR percentage at +0x370 is emitted at 0x140f806f0, so open question 7 / inference list can be promoted.
- 0x140f50cb0 ends with a tail call to 0x140f84480(world, 1) after zeroing the finalisation counters; not mentioned in the report.
- 'CUSTOM_BANDIT_LEADER' helper 0x140936ae0 runs in sub-step 11 (call at 0x140f6e50c), not in the sub-step-12 entity-year block as the claim-17 wording implies.
- The river generator 0x140f772f0 is called at 0x140f4f1d6 inside the stage-5 block, so 'Running rivers...' (shown from stage 3) leads the work by two stages, not one; the 'k+1' rule in section 2.4/claim 20 is a tendency, not a rule.
- The 100 ms stepper loop also has a GetTickCount wraparound guard (cmp eax,edi; jb at 0x140f505c4) - harmless, but the sketch in section 5 omits it.

### Open questions (carried from the report)

- Identity of the candidate vectors 0x1424f3ef0 / 0x1424f3f08 / 0x1424f3f20 and the per-tile flag 171 used by sub-steps 3-5 (what the per-year placement budget actually places).
- Internal loop structure of sub-step 12 (entity vs historical-figure iteration) - only labelled by callee strings.
- What the RNG-only passes 0x140f74cb0 (stage 8) and 0x140f74120 (sub-step 13) perturb.
- Writer of 0x1424f3e38 (total megabeasts used by the stop test) - only one RIP-relative read exists.
- Labels for the eight post-finalisation calls on 0x1423cb3e0 and the finish helper's per-entity call 0x140995a60.
- UI row layout: whether 'Recounting legends...' (row ebx) and the placer phase label (fixed row r13d=5) share a row.
- Whether NAME_SEED (+0x60) is ever pushed as a stream in the worldgen pipeline.
- Exact code form of the 53.07 complete-ocean-edge fix (check located at 0x140f4d5e2-0x140f4d669) and of the 53.09 pool-creature rule in the wildlife importer.

## gap_stage6_salinity_temperature_pass

**Title:** Stage-6 terrain finalisation: salinity rings, elevation-temperature correction, rainfall clamp, vegetation recompute (+ identify 0x140f74cb0)  
**Report:** analyst report `gap_stage6_salinity_temperature_pass.md` (session working file, not committed; the ledger is the adjudicated record)  

**Summary:** Stage-6 tick 0 of the DF 53.16 map generator (0x140f4f1db-0x140f4fb99) is a near-deterministic terrain finalisation: Mountain-Slope, a 3x3 volcano-cone bump (the only RNG), river-valley flattening to the mean of bank tiles clamped 100..149, the orographic sweep, Mountain-Slope again, then one per-tile loop applying temp -= (elev-100)^2/1000 for land, a squash of temperatures above the TEMPERATURE-max parameter (param+0x254 = 0x1424f391c) into [Tmax, Tmax+25], and a wet cap temp <= 166-rain for rain >= 66; vegetation is recomputed (rain, or rain/12 when freezing - a correction to /6 in two reports) and salinity seeded 100 ocean / 0 land, then two 8-connected ring passes give 66 and 33. Salinity is Chebyshev distance to coast; lakes touching ocean are salt, two away brackish, else fresh; salt swamps/mangroves only on coast tiles; all before the second region build; no evilness/drainage/savagery writes. Side task: 0x140f74cb0 initialises a per-tile weather/uphill-direction layer and six world random seeds.

### Refuted

- None.

### Skeptic extra findings

- Volcano pass: the centre-tile raise (max(hi+10+random(5), height), cap 250) and the peak-height update (+10+random(21), cap 280) are applied to EVERY element of the world+0x280 vector; flag bit 0 only gates the 3x3 neighbour raise and the volcanism>=90 floor. The algorithm sketch and summary should move those two steps outside the is_volcano condition.
- Stage-6 later ticks: the slice worker is 0x140f8b7c0 (geology/mineral builder; it increments 0x1424e5c10 at 0x140f8d9c7 and is re-run while a 0x140fb3670/0x140fb36f0 timer stays below 50), and 0x140f8a020 (feature-layer/cavern builder per geology_underground.md) runs once when the counter reaches 0x1424e5c14. Open question 6 should target 0x140f8b7c0; its decomp (decomp/140f8b7c0.c) shows no write to tile+76 or tile+54.
- Side task 0x140f74cb0: the low nibble is written only for tiles with elevation <= 102; highland local maxima and tiles with elevation 103..124 receive no direction bits (the else-branch begins with 'if (elev > 0x66) continue').
- Orographic sweep detail missed: 'prev' is initialised to the elevation of the upwind edge tile and that edge column is never processed (the loop's first iteration is skipped by cmp eax,width / jge at 0x140f4f786), so width-1 tiles per row are swept; the uplift adds 10*credit using the credit BEFORE increment, so the first charging tile adds 0.
- OROGRAPHIC_PRECIPITATION at param+0x4a8 can be upgraded from 'consistent with token order' to directly verified: the [WORLD_GEN] writer emits the 'OROGRAPHIC_PRECIPITATION:' string (0x141741048) at 0x140f818f8 and reads [rbp+0x4a8] at 0x140f81904.
- 0x140071ec0 is random(n) = (0x140eb1820() mod 0x7fffffff) / (0x7fffffff/n + 1) (returns 0 for n<=1); it draws from the same world-gen MT19937 stream 0x140eb1820 as the side task (0x9908b0df constants at 0x140eb195b/99b/9d9 and xmm table 0x141786a20), not from the 0x1405xxxxx wrapper family.
- The generator's angr decompilation decomp/140f4a790.c is empty ('no codegen'); all generator-body evidence in this report rests on disassembly, which reproduces correctly.
- The PUBLIC DFHack df-structures citations (world_mountain_peak layout, world_data unnamed int16 run) could not be fetched in this session (github.com/raw/jsdelivr unreachable); they remain unverified public context cited from memory, as the report itself flags for world_data.
- Claim 16's only untraced link is the semantics of the elev<100 branch in decomp/140f53750.c line 246 (frontier tiles below sea level vs lake members); the rest of the chain (carving clamp, stage-6 edits never lowering below 100, Mountain-Slope writing only +0x30) checks out.

### Open questions (carried from the report)

- What sets tile flag bit 15 and what it means (river tiles exempt from valley flattening, counted as bank, eligible for the volcano bump).
- Who consumes the tile+0x48 uphill-direction nibbles and the tile+0x40 = 3 state written by 0x140f74cb0; the weather/cloud-drift reading is inferred, no reader was found by pattern search.
- Why temperatures >= 150 are exempt from the wet cap (unreachable unless TEMPERATURE max >= 125).
- Whether river biomes (RIVER_*_BRACKISHWATER tokens) take their band from the world-tile salinity at region-detail realisation time.
- What the stage-8 callee 0x140f5c390 does with the per-tile salinity (>=66/>=33) and savagery (>=66) bands (per-region flags for population placement is the natural guess).
- Whether the mineral slicer 0x140f8a020 (later stage-6 ticks) touches any climate field; the generator itself has no later +0x36/+0x4c access but the slicer body was not inspected.

## gap_name_generation_and_seed_streams

**Title:** Name generation from language symbol sets, and which seed (SEED/HISTORY/NAME/CREATURE) drives which RNG stream  
**Report:** analyst report `gap_name_generation_and_seed_streams.md` (session working file, not committed; the ledger is the adjudicated record)  

**Summary:** One 6 KB routine (0x140a8f8e0) names everything in worldgen. It takes a language_name record (first_name, nickname, words[7], parts_of_speech[7], language, name type, has_name; layout verified), a language (-1 = random usable translation), a name type (0-54, ten form bodies) and two word tables: A for the head noun/first name, B for modifiers. A word table is six (word, part-of-speech) position lists compiled once per symbol recipe into word_table[2][68] by 0x140d29c80 from language_SYM.txt (select-noun, select-modifier, cull lists); the 16-bit usage flags route words into positions at build time. Regions use NAME_<kind> nouns with neutral/evil/good modifier recipes; islands, peaks, volcanoes, caves have their own symbols; rivers use an unrestricted pool. Seeds: NAME_SEED only names the world; SEED drives the map stream (incl. the map-stage Lua generation); HISTORY_SEED scopes civ placement; CREATURE_SEED feeds the placer's Lua generation; a 3-value pairing push makes coordinate sub-streams.

### Refuted

- **`gap_name_generation_and_seed_streams-21`** (analyst status *verified*, confidence 0.90): The SEED stream stays active through the whole map generator and is popped at the end of stage 9 (0x140f50587) just before the stage counter 0x1424e5a50 increments; the map generator's Lua generate call passes a copy of SEED, which the Lua entry pushes/pops as a nested stream.
  - Evidence: [BIN:func 0x140f4a790 @0x140f4aae8, 0x140f50587]; [BIN:func 0x140eca3a0 @0x140eca458 push, 0x140eca84b pop]
  - Verdict: refuted: The pop at 0x140f50587 and the SEED-copy Lua call (0x140f4aac0-0x140f4aae8, nested push 0x140eca458 / inline pop 0x140eca84b) exist, but under cmp r10w,0x9 the stage first runs the final checker 0x140f6b660 (0x140f505aa) whose only stream-touching callee is the placer, the placer's six pops are all rejection exits and its success path reaches the single epilogue 0x140f6b558 without popping, the final checker then sets guard 0x1426388e8 so later ticks do post-processing and pop, and a global inventory of every call-based and inlined depth write finds no other pop reachable from these functions, so 0x140f50587 pops the HISTORY_SEED stream (restoring SEED) and SEED itself is popped by the stage worker at stage 10 (0x140df089a under cmp cx,0xa).

### Skeptic extra findings

- Stream lifecycle is misread: stage 9 runs the final checker -> placer before the pop at 0x140f50587; the placer pops only on its six rejection exits (each paired with a map_rejection_log write), so on success 0x140f50587 pops HISTORY_SEED and the stage worker's pop at 0x140df089a (stage 10) pops SEED.
- Stage-9 post-processing after the placer (calls 0x14143e1d0, 0x14143bea0, 0x14143c460, 0x14143dda0, 0x14143e020, 0x141509e90, 0x141508be0, 0x1415088d0 and the 0x140f71800 loop) therefore runs on the HISTORY_SEED stream, not SEED.
- Cave names (k=14 calls) are generated before the HISTORY_SEED push, so they draw from the SEED stream; the report's Inferences paragraph wrongly groups cave names with the HISTORY_SEED stream.
- Reset routine 0x140eb17a0 zeroes depth/selector and seeds slot 0 from the tick counter; after the stage-10 pop nothing in this RNG family re-pushes a seed, so later worldgen stages using it run on a time-seeded base stream unless they use the other MT family (0x14050d110 wrappers) — worth flagging as an open question.
- Generator tail has an extra retry trigger the report missed: if parts[5]==1 and parts[6]==1 (both 'The X' and 'of Y' slots plural nouns) the name is regenerated (0x140a9001b-0x140a9002a).
- For name types 8 and 9 the slot re-initialisation is skipped when the record already holds content (0x140a8f9a5-0x140a8f9be), i.e. the generator can extend an existing name.
- Open question 1 is already answered by the cited disassembly: the subregion builder passes language -1 (mov edx,0xffffffff at 0x140f5289c), as do landmass, evil/good renaming, river and brook calls.
- 4-letter symbol names (EVIL, GOOD, HOLY, UGLY) fall below the strings -n 5 threshold; the report's k=0-2 recipes are wrong: k=0/1 cull DOMESTIC, UGLY, SUBORDINATE and EVIL, and k=2 puts EVIL and DEATH in the noun-select list and culls only DOMESTIC (the 'artifact' label for k=2 is unverified).
- 0x1407780e0 is a third tagged push (inline three-value pairing) used at 0x140fb9537/0x140fb9a54; the feature-layer builder thus has four three-value pushes, four tagged value pushes and seven pops, and its untagged three-value pushes use (index+1)*10000.
- The HISTORY_SEED push is guarded by BYTE [0x1424e619b]==0 (the placer is time-sliced and re-entered; the flag is incremented at 0x140f6624c), and the NAME_SEED/world-name block in world init is guarded by [0x1424f3780]==0.
- Civ-side calls use entity/object-owned modifier tables as table B (entity +0x3d0 in 0x14098dd90, rbp+0xd0 at 0x1405d8543), not T[1][k]; only table A comes from the global word_table array.
- Translation word-string vector begins at +0x50 (end pointer at +0x58); the report's '+0x58' is the end pointer.
- Many pops are inlined (direct writes to 0x142354110), e.g. the abort handler 0x140deb2a0 ('World generation manually aborted'), the Lua entry and world init; a call-based inventory of 0x140eb1bc0 undercounts pops.
- Claim 01's evidence list conflates direct call sites with k-wrapper callers (0x140f4d01d, 0x140f4e129, 0x140f6138e call 0x140a8f880) and with the function 0x14098dd90 (which holds 19 generator calls and is itself called from 16 sites).

### Open questions (carried from the report)

- Which language index the subregion builder passes when naming regions (a civ's language or -1); only peaks/volcanoes were verified as -1.
- Exact semantics of the three values at the feature-layer pushes (which world-data field at +0x2c/+0x30 and which per-block array feeds the pairing seed).
- The literal the seed strings are compared against in world init (assumed 'RANDOM' or empty) and how the replacement random seed is generated.
- Whether the world_gen_param writer 0x140f80330 writes the parameter seed strings or the resolved last_used_* seeds held by the worldgen-status serializer 0x141522140.
- Detailed form logic of the bodies for name types 3, 7, 11, 48 and 49/51 (only their table/position calls were sampled).
- The 53.16 language_name_type names for types 19 (peak/volcano), 20 (river/brook) and 37/40/41 (site-style variants).
- Location of the English/native name renderer; the ' of the ' / ' the ' strings are shared by many text builders and did not isolate it.
